Against the task's targets (per 30 s):

| target | before (median) | after (median) | met? |
|---|---|---|---|
| SI-SDRi >= +2 dB | -1.57 | -1.93 | **no**: not reachable by any gate on this clip (below) |
| true dropouts <= 0.5 s | 5.42 | 1.50 | **no**, 3.6x fewer; the rest is the lip ASD scoring the talking target below 0.05-0.2 |
| leak <= -12 dB | -10.2 | -9.9 | **no**: a gate cannot duck the other talker while the target talks over it |
| DNSMOS OVRL >= input + 0.3 (1.86) | 1.67 | 1.97 | **yes** in 2 of 3 runs (1.84 in the third) |

Why a gate cannot reach +2 dB / -12 dB here: on the clean stems the target is active in 98 % of 20 ms frames and both talkers in 76 % (other alone: 1 %). A whole-band gain can only act on time, so the best possible gate on this clip is "open almost always" (≈ the natural chain, `dfn_ll` alone: -1.9 dB SI-SDRi, because DFN3 keeps both voices and adds its own distortion on overlapped speech) and every dB it ducks during overlap costs the target the same dB. The both-talking partial gain is therefore capped at -12 dB and only applied when another face is over the on-threshold. Isolation under overlap needs the TSE tiers (enrolled / AV Focus HQ). Natural (`dfn_ll`, no gate) is unchanged by this task, as expected. The onset-clip metric sees only one onset in this looped clip (the target barely pauses), so it is a guard for other clips, not evidence here. Tuning was done offline by replaying the recorded telemetry (`p_speaking` per face at 10 Hz) of the "before" sessions through `dfn_ll>gate` with each parameter set (dropouts 5.5 -> 0.9 s in that replay at equal leak; the live runs differ because ASD timing differs run to run).

**Re-measured on the T-097 scene clip** (main replaced the looped SIR-0 clip with a 30 s two-face scene: target alone, then alternating turns, then overlap; input SI-SDR +1.95 dB). After merging T-093 the gate ducks to `duck_gain(s) = max(room_gain(s), -30 dB)` (T-093's -3 dB per 10 slider points, floored at -30 dB, so suppression 100 is still not digital silence). Same command as above, laptop-a, 2026-10-06, load averages 4.6-7.9 (other agents running):

| run | attention | focus_hq SI-SDRi dB | leak dB | true dropouts s | onset clips | DNSMOS OVRL | target picked | natural SI-SDRi / leak / dropouts / OVRL |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `demo_regress_20261006-130006` | server default | -4.01 | -12.2 | 6.30 | 5 / 14 | 2.68 | right, then switched to the other talker at 12.1 s | +0.06 / -8.3 / 0.00 / 2.71 |
| `demo_regress_20261006-130115` | server default | -3.96 (replay; first -5.44) | -12.3 | 6.30 (first 7.90) | 5 / 14 | 2.69 | same switch | +0.06 / -8.3 / 0.00 / 2.71 |
| `demo_regress_20261006-130257` | server default | -3.97 (replay; first -8.29) | -12.2 | 6.30 (first 10.42) | 5 / 14 | 2.69 | same switch | +0.06 / -8.3 / 0.00 / 2.71 |
| `demo_regress_20261006-130709` | sticky (`--attention sticky`) | -8.53 | -10.5 | 10.26 | 6 / 14 | 2.75 | **wrong from 1 s** (the more central face, x 0.58, before ASD warmed up) | (not run) |
| `demo_regress_20261006-130746` | sticky | -8.39 | -10.5 | 10.70 | 7 / 14 | 2.73 | wrong from 1 s | (not run) |
| `demo_regress_20261006-130823` | sticky | **+1.66** | **-14.8** | **0.12** | 1 / 14 | 2.77 | **right** (track 1, left) all 30 s | (not run) |

What it says: on the scene clip the gate itself does what the task asks once it follows the right face (run `130823`: leak -14.8 dB <= -12, true dropouts 0.12 s <= 0.5 s, SI-SDRi +1.66 dB, 0.34 dB short of +2). Every bad run is **target selection**, not the gate: ASD separates the two faces cleanly (p ~ 0.99 vs 0.03 in single-talker turns), but the replayed target sits at the left edge (x 0.05) while the other talker is near the centre (x 0.58), so the attention picks the central face in the first second (2 of 3 sticky runs) or, with the default 2 s hold, switches to the other talker during their first long turn (all 3 default runs). A real wearer faces the target, which is what attention's centre prior assumes. The fix is in target selection or in the clip (a lock, or the target framed at the centre), not in `gate.py`. Targets: SI-SDRi >= +2 dB **not met** (best run +1.66 dB, median of the sticky runs -8.4 dB because of the wrong-target runs); dropouts <= 0.5 s and leak <= -12 dB **met in the right-target run only**; DNSMOS OVRL vs input + 0.3: (not measured: the scorer does not report the input's DNSMOS for the new clip; outputs are 2.68-2.77 against 2.71 for natural). A before/after on this clip for the old gate was queued (main snapshot, same flags) but did not finish inside this run.


## GPU demo preset on the iLab A100 (T-086, 2026-10-06)

### Enrolled window/hop sweep
iLab `rlab2`, NVIDIA A100-PCIE-40GB, SLURM job 432518 (bake-off inside the server's allocation with
`srun --overlap --jobid=432518`), 16 `av_full` two-face clips per row, enroll `self`, mock transcriber.
In `~/hearing-remote`: `.venv/bin/python -m eval.bakeoff --set eval/fixtures/generated/av_full --layout two_face
--candidates enrolled --windows 0.5,0.4,0.3,0.25 --limit 16 --enroll self --transcriber mock --partial --out
eval/results/t086a` (run `20261006-201058`), and the same with `--windows 0.5 --hop 0.25 --out eval/results/t086b`
(hop = 0.25 x window, run `20261006-201133`). Raw JSON stays on iLab. Delay = window + hop (threaded tse), plus
the 200 ms playout buffer and the tunnel RTT through the laptop's `remote` stage. Utilisation = compute p95 / hop.

| window s | hop s | SI-SDRi dB | compute p50 / p95 ms | p95 / hop | RTF | algorithmic delay ms | through the laptop (+200 ms buffer) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 0.5 | 0.25 | +9.73 | 68.5 / 197.9 | 79 % | 0.308 | 750 | 950 |
| 0.4 | 0.2 | +8.34 | 68.7 / 193.3 | 97 % | 0.368 | 600 | 800 |
| 0.3 | 0.15 | +7.57 | 68.4 / 170.4 | 114 % | 0.483 | 450 | 650 |
| 0.25 | 0.125 | +6.00 | 68.1 / 73.7 | 59 % | 0.571 | 375 | 575 |
| **0.5** | **0.125** | **+10.68** | **69.4 / 85.3** | **68 %** | 0.547 | **625** | **825** |

Pick: **0.5 s / 0.125 s**, the only row at >= +10 dB with p95 < 70 % of the hop, and lower delay than 0.5 / 0.25.
Shorter windows lose 1.4-3.7 dB. The p95 column is noisy on this shared node: the 0.5 / 0.25 row's 198 ms p95
is 2-3 slow windows per clip, not the steady state (p50 is flat at 68-69 ms at every window). Now in
`config/tiers-remote.yaml` (the ladder `gpu_up.sh ilab` serves) and `config/tiers.yaml` `remote.enrolled`.

Tunnel RTT from laptop-a (`bash scripts/gpu_up.sh probe`, 2026-10-06 20:15 EDT, via the `cm-ilab` SSH
ControlMaster to `rlab2`): **p50 6.1 ms, p95 18.9 ms**. Which network the laptop was on at the time was not
recorded by the probe.

### Live replay through the A100: window and ladder order
`swarm-lock --exclusive -- env HEARING_HQ_BACKEND=remote HEARING_REMOTE_URL=ws://127.0.0.1:<port>/ws/session
.venv/bin/python scripts/demo_regress.py --modes focus_hq --seconds 30 --no-free --clip <main>/eval/fixtures/demo/demo-mf-easy`,
target locked, 3 runs each, laptop-a (M4 Pro, load average 3.4-7.2). The 0.5 / 0.125 server and the AV-first
server were extra `hearing serve` processes in the same job (ports 18766 / 18767, `tiers(config=...)` with the
spec below), stopped afterwards. Rows marked (replay) are demo_regress's automatic second session.

| remote ladder | enrolled window / hop | runs (SI-SDRi dB) | leak dB | DNSMOS OVRL | true dropouts s | delay ms | first isolated s |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| enrolled > AV > gate (old default, :18765) | 0.5 / 0.25 (`speed` forwarded) | +4.19, +6.33, +4.12 (+5.75 replay) | -15.2, -10.4, -22.8 | 2.83, 2.84, 2.68 | 0.62, 0.00, 0.86 | 950-1075 | 4.1-5.0 |
| **enrolled > AV > gate (T-086 default)** | **0.5 / 0.125** | **+5.84, +5.69, +6.87** | -9.0, -11.5, -16.2 | 2.41, 2.87, 2.33 | 0.00, 0.52, 0.00 | **950** | 4.5-7.4 |
| AV > enrolled > gate | 0.5 / 0.125 | +7.10, +2.71, +9.00 (+9.11 replay) | -18.9, -18.7, -17.0 | 2.41, 2.86, 2.51 | 0.00, 0.12, 0.00 | **3200** | 5.3-10.4 |

Sessions: `regress-20261006-201439`, `-201517`, `-201554` (old), `-201715`, `-201752`, `-201829` (T-086),
`-201949`, `-202026`, `-202136` (AV first), under `results/sessions/`.

- **Order.** Enrolled first stays: AV first buys a median +1.2 dB with a 2.25 s longer delay and a 2.7 dB spread
  between runs, and its DNSMOS is no better. AV stays in the remote ladder for the cold start and for same-gender
  pairs (the `same_bucket` guard skips enrolled there, T-070/T-075). Same-gender pairs were not re-measured here.
- **The cited regression** (`demo_regress_20261006-192903`, "focus_hq 203 ticks, enrolled never promoted"):
  the top-level telemetry `tier` stays `focus_hq` while remote audio plays (CONTRACTS.md, T-108); the remote
  ladder's `tiers.active` in that session was enrolled for 163 of 219 ticks (locked run) and AV only in the
  free-selection run (39 ticks), whose -10 dB came from the target switch, not the ladder.
- **Window.** 0.5 / 0.125 scores higher than 0.5 / 0.25 live (median +5.84 vs +4.19 dB). Its delay is 950 ms,
  not the nominal 825: under the live session (vision, ASD and the AV tier share the GPU) the threaded stage
  plays one hop late (remote `tiers.delay_ms` 687.5 at 6 s, 750 from 15 s). The 0.5 / 0.25 server ran
  1075 ms (one 0.25 s hop late) in two of three runs.

### Perceptual post-filter on the isolated voice
The three old-default A100 sessions above (slider 100, so the played output is the isolated target), post-filtered
offline by streaming each `out.wav` through the stage in 10 ms chunks at suppression 1.0, then rescored with
`scripts/session_score.py --dnsmos dnsmos` (same scorer, same alignment).

| post-filter | SI-SDRi dB (3 runs, mean) | DNSMOS SIG | BAK | OVRL | true dropouts s |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| none | +4.19, +6.33, +4.12 (**+4.88**) | 3.44 | 3.28 | **2.78** | 0.62, 0.00, 0.86 |
| `dfn` (DeepFilterNet3, 33 ms) | +4.06, +6.45, +4.40 (**+4.97**) | 3.42 | 3.95 | **3.08** | 1.20, 0.54, 1.36 |
| `dfn_ll` (13 ms) | +2.81, +4.80, +3.20 (**+3.60**) | 3.46 | 4.05 | **3.16** | 1.90, 0.88, 1.78 |

`dfn` passes the bar (OVRL +0.30, SI-SDRi does not drop); `dfn_ll` fails it (-1.28 dB). `dfn` is the option
`remote.enrolled_dfn` in `config/tiers.yaml` (`tse(...)>dfn>mixer`: the tse residual still reaches the mixer),
**not** the default: it was measured offline, it adds 0.5-0.6 s of true dropouts (DFN removes quiet target onsets),
and a CPU DFN on the GPU box's event loop is what put the remote behind real time in T-075.

## Remote dfn_ll cold-start tier + CPUs per job (T-115, 2026-10-06)

Not shipped: `config/tiers-remote.yaml` keeps the pre-T-115 ladder (gate tier `'gate'`, audio-only `'passthrough'`, `min_switch_s: 3.0`). A/B on job 432663 (A100, rlab2), both servers restarted from this branch with `ILAB_REUSE=432663 REMOTE_PORT=18771/18772 scripts/gpu_up.sh ilab` (remote file checked by md5: gate side `28756acd...` = the shipped file), laptop `HEARING_HQ_BACKEND=remote`, each run `swarm-lock --exclusive -- bash scripts/demo_regress.sh --modes focus_hq --seconds 60 --no-baseline --no-free --dnsmos dnsmos` on `demo-mf-easy` (load 4.3-7.6). Each run is a first replay plus, when `demo_regress` flagged a regression, its re-replay (`a / b`). Cold start = DNSMOS OVRL of the first 7 s of `out.wav` (`eval.dnsmos.get_dnsmos("dnsmos")`; the input scores 1.44 there).

| ladder | run | cold-start DNSMOS 0-7 s | whole-run DNSMOS | SI-SDRi | leak | true dropouts | measured delay | tier sw | first isolated |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| dfn_ll bridge (`dfn_ll>gate(hold_ms=1000,max_duck_db=20)>mixer`, audio-only `dfn_ll`) | 1 (225252) | 2.32 / 2.29 | 2.58 / 2.50 | -4.4 / -2.7 dB | -6.8 / -8.0 dB | 0.42 / 0.44 s | 1500 ms | 64 / 77 | 0.8 / 2.1 s |
| dfn_ll bridge | 2 (225506) | 2.28 / 2.16 | 2.43 / 2.67 | -2.0 / +2.2 dB | -8.4 / -8.4 dB | 0.38 / 0.26 s | 1500 / 1700 ms | 55 / 11 | 1.2 / 1.3 s |
| dfn_ll bridge | 3 (225721) | 2.47 / 2.19 | 2.64 / 2.51 | -2.8 / -2.2 dB | -7.1 / -10.2 dB | 0.28 / 0.62 s | 1750 ms | 72 / 80 | 2.9 / 4.1 s |
| shipped (`gate`, `passthrough`) | 1 (230010) | 1.95 / 1.74 | 2.18 / 2.40 | -3.9 / +2.6 dB | -3.8 / -12.7 dB | 0.42 / 0.32 s | 200 / 3200 ms | 10 / 6 | 2.0 / 0.2 s |
| shipped | 2 (230226) | 1.73 | 2.37 | +4.6 dB | -18.4 dB | 0.00 s | 3200 ms | 4 | 7.8 s |
| shipped | 3 (230337) | 1.56 | 2.64 | +4.4 dB | -16.7 dB | 0.48 s | 3200 ms | 3 | 7.8 s |

The dfn_ll bridge reaches the 2.3 cold-start target on 4 of 6 replays (2.16-2.47) but costs the whole run: SI-SDRi -4.4 to +2.2 dB against +2.6 to +4.6 dB on the shipped ladder, leak about -8 dB against -12.7 to -18.4 dB, 11-80 tier switches against 3-10. Cause: `hearing/fusion/tiers.py` feeds every tier every chunk (so a tier is warm when needed), so the remote session runs dfn_ll on its CPU for the whole run, not only during the cold start; with it the alignment shows the output 1.5-2.2 s behind (the box falls behind real time, as with T-075's two CPU DFN instances), Enrolled barely plays and the ladder flips between AV and the gate. Restricting the tier to audio-only would not help (the tier would still run every chunk); a dfn_ll that stops after the first Enrolled needs a ladder change (a tier that retires) or GPU DFN, left as a follow-up. Earlier T-115 rows (222701-223127, cold start 2.32-2.35) ran on a server with a stale `min_switch_s: 10.0` and are superseded by this A/B. CPU flags: see TESTING_GUIDE section 7 (all rejected, the job already sees 96 cores; the bottleneck is the one Python session process, not the allocation).

## Ladder idle tiers suspended (T-116, 2026-10-07)

`hearing/fusion/tiers.py` now runs only the tiers that may play next (the playing one, every better one, the fallback below it, tiers needing `healthy`, `remote` tiers); the others are suspended and primed with the input they missed (at most 0.5 s) before they run or play. A/B on laptop-a (M4 Pro, MPS) with the same code, toggled by `HEARING_TIERS_IDLE=run` (old: every tier every chunk) vs the default `suspend`, interleaved, each `swarm-lock --exclusive -- bash scripts/demo_regress.sh --modes focus_hq --seconds 60 --no-baseline --no-free --dnsmos auto` on `demo-mf-easy`, target locked, load 2.4-7.7 (1-min average). Ladder ms/hop = `telemetry.tiers.hop_ms` (new in T-116: the ladder's own compute per 10 ms hop over the last 500 chunks, priming included; the threaded enrolled tse runs in its worker thread and is not in it), median over the session's ticks; enrolled utilisation = the tse `utilisation` (compute p50 / hop), median over ticks; overruns/late = the tse counters at the end. On the Mac ladder the idle tier is audio-only (`dfn_ll`): suspended in 454-459 of ~590 ticks (whenever enrolled plays with the gate as fallback).

| idle | run (session) | ladder ms/hop p50 / p95 | enrolled utilisation | overruns / late | SI-SDRi | leak | DNSMOS OVRL | true dropouts | tier sw | enrolled share |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| run (before) | 1 (000746) | 1.10 / 1.84 | 0.489 | 1 / 1 | +5.56 dB | -8.8 dB | 2.65 | 0.00 s | 2 | 77 % |
| run (before) | 2 (001007) | 1.30 / 2.13 | 0.525 | 1 / 1 | +5.62 dB | -8.1 dB | 2.70 | 0.00 s | 2 | 77 % |
| run (before) | 3 (001230) | 1.27 / 2.04 | 0.526 | 1 / 1 | +5.20 dB | -8.4 dB | 2.65 | 0.00 s | 2 | 77 % |
| suspend (after) | 1 (000856) | 0.26 / 0.70 | 0.512 | 1 / 1 | +5.93 dB | -8.1 dB | 2.70 | 0.00 s | 2 | 77 % |
| suspend (after) | 2 (001119) | 0.27 / 0.77 | 0.523 | 1 / 1 | +6.22 dB | -9.7 dB | 2.66 | 0.00 s | 2 | 78 % |
| suspend (after) | 3 (001341) | 0.27 / 0.74 | 0.518 | 1 / 1 | +5.63 dB | -8.8 dB | 2.65 | 0.00 s | 2 | 77 % |

Ladder compute per hop -79 % (p50 1.10-1.30 -> 0.26-0.27 ms, p95 1.84-2.13 -> 0.70-0.77 ms); enrolled overruns unchanged (1 each: the cold first window); scores not worse (SI-SDRi +5.20..+5.62 -> +5.63..+6.22 dB, leak -8.1..-8.8 -> -8.1..-9.7 dB, DNSMOS 2.65-2.70 both, no dropouts, 2 switches each). The enrolled tse utilisation does not move (0.49-0.53 both): on the M4 Pro at this load the enrolled worker thread was not starved by `dfn_ll`, so the Mac gain is main-loop CPU, not enrolled compute. The case T-115 hit (a CPU-bound A100 session running dfn_ll beside enrolled) is where the freed CPU should matter; that A/B needs a GPU tunnel (none in T-116), so `config/tiers-remote.yaml` keeps the bridge off. Design note: a per-stage "shadow" mode (consume input, skip inference) was not built: the only suspendable stages on either ladder are `dfn_ll`, `gate`, `mixer` and `passthrough`, whose state is fully rebuilt by 0.5 s of replay (unit tests check the output is sample-identical to the always-running ladder through aligned, splice and dip switches); tse tiers (enrollment buffers, health that only moves while fed) are never suspended.

## T-117: Synthetic first-word latency and Text WER, end to end (`scripts/judge_check.py`)
`demo-mf-easy`, 30 s replay per mode with the manifest's target locked, laptop-a (M4 Pro, MPS), `swarm-lock --exclusive`, load average 4.0 before, local faster-whisper ASR + Kokoro (no `ELEVENLABS_API_KEY`), `synth_feed` auto (mixture). Command: `.venv/bin/python scripts/judge_check.py --no-hw` (rows 6-7).

| Mode | Metric | Value |
|---|---|---|
| Synthetic (Kokoro) | first-word latency, final caption `t1` -> first non-silent sample in `out.wav` | p50 **1.70 s**, p95 3.07 s, 13 phrases of 14 finals (bar 2.5 s with Kokoro: PASS; the 1.5 s ElevenLabs bar is not measured: no key) |
| Synthetic | telemetry `synth_phrase_p50` at the end of the run | 4797 ms (counts the queue behind earlier phrases) |
| Synthetic | empty / `. . .` captions, voice-bucket changes | 0, none reported (the replay has no apparent-voice estimate in telemetry) |
| Text | caption WER vs the target transcript (manifest `transcript`) | 92 % (71 caption words vs 78 target words, 14 finals, 0 empty) |

The end-to-end first word is well above the 400 ms TTS-only figure: it holds the end-of-phrase pause, Whisper compute and Kokoro's first chunk. The Text WER is dominated by Whisper on GRID command phrases, not by the other talker.

## T-120: Name Call (camera-free, "say my name"), 2026-10-07
laptop-a (M4 Pro, MPS; faster-whisper `base.en` CPU int8; ECAPA + MossFormer2 enrolled tier at the Mac's 1.0 s / 0.5 s), each run under `swarm-lock --exclusive`.
Scene (`eval/namecall_eval.py scene`, 36 s, no video): babble 10 dB SNR; a GRID male talks from 0 s; a Kokoro `af_heart` voice says
"Aryan, hey, can you hear me over here?" at 7.0 s (name ends ~7.6 s) and keeps talking, overlapping the GRID talker at SIR 0 dB.
Replayed through `Session` in 20 ms frames at real time, `mode: name_call`, `hello video: false`; scored from Isolated on
with the output aligned by cross-correlation (1.5 s, the enrolled tier's delay).

| Run (load avg) | Name detected after its end | Name -> Isolated | SI-SDRi from Isolated | Other talker in output | Wrong target | "Target quiet" while target talks |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 (4.7) | 0.41 s | 2.8 s | +10.09 dB | -18.9 dB | 0.83 s / 10 s | 22.6 s (heard threshold 0.5, fixed below) |
| 2 (4.5) | 0.40 s | 2.8 s | +10.09 dB | -18.9 dB | 0.83 s / 10 s | same |
| 3 (3.9), heard threshold 0.25, re-target guard | 0.42 s | **2.3 s** | **+10.08 dB** | -18.9 dB | **0.81 s / 10 s** | 0.0 s |

False triggers (`eval/namecall_eval.py false-triggers`, load 7.5): **0** in 5.06 min of speech without the name (153 s of GRID
sentences + 150 s of Kokoro in four voices, room floor -55 dBFS), Whisper windows at RTF 0.26 (2.5 s windows every 1 s,
~330 ms each with temperature fallback off; 1.1-1.6 s each with it on).
Bars (acceptance): Isolated <= 4 s after the name (2.3-2.8 s), wrong target <= 1 s / 10 s (0.81-0.83), false triggers <= 1 per 5 min (0).

`demo_regress --modes name_call --seconds 30 --no-baseline` (demo-mf-easy, camera-free, name = "green", a word only the target
says; 3 runs, load 3.4-5.0, final code in the last): Whisper never writes the target's first "green" (3.8 s, GRID "bin green by b
two please" in babble) as a match; it hears the second one ("set green at i seven soon", name ~19.9 s) as "That green eye, 7 -2."
2.1 s after the name (the name sat at the end of one 2.5 s window and was caught by the next), the pill goes Isolated at 24.3 s,
and the row scores SI-SDRi **+0.28 dB**, leak -9.4 dB, 0.00 s true dropouts over the whole 30 s (Natural until 24 s; the
`first isolated s` column is `-` because session_score reads the camera acquisition state). A later "green" spoken in the
overlap (ECAPA similarity of the mixed clip to the target 0.15) no longer re-targets the same person: the enrolled tier heard the
target during it. Run 1 (before that guard) re-targeted there.

## T-123: Name Call false-trigger guards (match level x Whisper name prompt), 2026-10-07

`.venv/bin/python -m eval.namecall_eval matrix` (laptop-a M4 Pro, mps, Whisper `base.en`, load 6.2, not under swarm-lock:
counts do not depend on load; RTF does). Sets through `NameCaller` inline (`dfn_ll` cleaner): **name** = 16 Kokoro sentences
(4 voices) saying "Aryan" (detected = sentences with a hit); **Ryan** = 16 sentences saying "Ryan" (false triggers);
**none** = the T-120 5.06 min set without the name (false triggers). Prompt = `initial_prompt` + `hotwords` = "Aryan.".

| Prompt | Match | Exclude | Name detected | "Ryan" set triggers | 5 min triggers | RTF |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| off | strict | - | 7/16 | 0/16 | 0 | 0.27 |
| off | **normal** (default) | (built-in Ryan, Brian, Bryan, Orion, Arya) | **14/16** | **0/16** | **0** | 0.27 |
| off | loose (T-120) | - | 14/16 | 14/16 | 0 | 0.27 |
| off | loose | Ryan | 14/16 | 0/16 | 0 | 0.27 |
| on | strict | - | 16/16 | 1/16 | 7 | 0.27 |
| on | normal | (built-in) | 16/16 | 1/16 | 7 | 0.27 |
| on | loose | - | 16/16 | 14/16 | 7 | 0.27 |
| on | loose | Ryan | 16/16 | 1/16 | 7 | 0.27 |

Without the prompt Whisper writes "Ariane"/"Arion" for 7 of the 16 names (strict misses them); with it every name is spelled
"Aryan", but it also writes "Aryan." into 7 windows of ordinary speech and 1 "Ryan" sentence: the prompt is a hallucination
source. **Defaults: `match: normal`, prompt off** (`namecall.DEFAULT_MATCH`, `DEFAULT_PROMPT`): the only cell with no false
trigger on either set and the best detection without the prompt. Results:
`eval/results/namecall_matrix_20261007-195243.json` (main checkout).

## T-131: Name Call guards (aliases + strict instead of the prompt; prompt-mode hit guard), 2026-10-08

`.venv/bin/python -m eval.namecall_eval matrix --aliases Ariane,Arion,Arian,Aurean,Aria,Ari` (laptop-a M4 Pro, mps, Whisper
`base.en` CPU int8, beam 1, temperature 0, load 17.6, not under swarm-lock: counts do not depend on load). Same three sets as
T-123 (16 "Aryan" sentences, 16 "Ryan" sentences, 5.06 min without the name). The aliases are the spellings Whisper writes for
Kokoro's "Aryan" without the prompt (`heard_name_like` / `name_windows_prompt_off` in the result). Guard (`namecall.guard_reject`,
on whenever the prompt is): >= 2 words besides the name, name word probability >= 0.1, segment `avg_logprob` > -1.0,
`no_speech_prob` < 0.6 (profile gate, ECAPA cosine >= 0.45, only with `$HEARING_PROFILES_DIR` profiles; not exercised here).

| Prompt | Match | Aliases | Guard | Name detected | "Ryan" set triggers | 5 min triggers |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| off | normal (T-123 default) | - | - | 14/16 | 0/16 | 0 |
| off | **strict** | **6 spellings** | - | **15/16** | **0/16** | **0** |
| off | normal | 6 spellings | - | 15/16 | 1/16 | 1 |
| on | strict | - | - | 16/16 | 1/16 | 7 |
| on | strict | - | **on** | **14/16** | **0/16** | **0** |
| on | strict | - | on, word p >= 0.5 (first run) | 3/16 | 0/16 | 0 |

The prompt-on false triggers are all prompt echoes: "Aryan." at word probability 0.00-0.01 with 0-1 other words (segment
`avg_logprob` -0.34 to -0.78, `no_speech_prob` 0.10-0.42, so those two gates never fire here). Real prompted names score
0.05-0.86, so the task's 0.5 word-probability bar kept only 3/16; 0.1 keeps 14/16. The one name aliases + strict misses is
"Aryan, your phone..." (written "RE in your phone."). With `normal` the short aliases ("Aria", "Ari") fuzzy-match ordinary
words ("are", "area", "Aaron"): use **strict** with aliases. Results: `eval/results/namecall_matrix_20261008-190933.json` (main checkout);
first run (3 aliases, guard p >= 0.5): `namecall_matrix_20261008-190238.json`.

Scene (`eval.namecall_eval scene --aliases Ariane,Arion,Arian,Aurean,Aria,Ari --match strict`, under `swarm-lock --exclusive`,
load 7.0 -> 7.4): Whisper wrote the name as "Ariane." ("I'm sorry. Ariane."), the alias caught it 0.40 s after the name's end,
name -> Isolated **2.9 s** (bar 4 s), SI-SDRi +10.09 dB, other talker -18.4 dB, wrong target 0.83 s / 10 s, 0 s "Target quiet".
`eval/results/namecall_scene_20261008-191256.json`.

Caption Whisper (`WhisperASR._transcribe`) on the same babble scene, 68 windows of 2.5 s, compute per window, two passes each,
same process, under `swarm-lock --exclusive` (load 8.5): temperature fallback (before) p50 **0.317 s**, p90 1.40 s, max 3.16 s;
`temperature=0.0` (now) p50 **0.313 s**, p90 **0.336 s**, max 0.366 s (T-120's bar: 0.33 s). Script: `.venv/bin/python -m eval.caption_latency`
(committed after the run; same code as the scratch script that produced these numbers).

## T-132: End-to-end trigger -> isolation with wrong-speaker routing (Name Call), 2026-10-08
**Go/no-go bar, fixed before the runs:** from Isolated on, wrong-speaker <= **5 %** of target-active time and no wrong-speaker
stretch > **1 s**, for the demo pair (mf).

`eval/pipeline_eval.py` replays 36 s scenes in 20 ms frames at real time through the live session path (in-process `Session`,
warm-up as `hearing serve`; `--url` replays through a server's `/ws/session`), `mode: name_call`, name "Aryan", no video. The
enrollment comes only from the name utterance inside the test recording (Name Call), and the scoring covers the rest of that
recording, which is disjoint speech. Talker A is Kokoro (male `am_michael`, female `af_heart`; it has to say the name in its own voice). It says the name at 7.0 s and
keeps talking (`namecall_eval.A_LINES`). B is a GRID talker (mf -> s4, mm -> s2, ff -> s7) at SIR 0 dB with babble at 10 dB SNR. In the
default "clear name" condition B pauses from 6.6 to 8.4 s; `--overlap` keeps B talking over the name. `mm-rt`: B is Kokoro `am_echo`,
who says "Aryan" at 28.0 s, so from then on B is the intended talker. **Wrong-speaker routing:** on 0.25 s windows aligned by
cross-correlation (1.5 s), a window where the intended talker is active (stem within 20 dB of its speech level) is wrong when the
other talker's share of the output (least-squares stem gains x stem RMS) is the larger one. After a re-target, A still playing
counts as wrong. Switches = re-targets the session logged / dominant-speaker flips. laptop-a (M4 Pro, MPS, Whisper `base.en`
CPU), each run under `swarm-lock --exclusive`.

| Scene (load avg) | Names hit | False / duplicate | Name end -> Isolated | Wrong from Isolated (s / %) | Longest wrong | Wrong from name end (s / %) | Re-targets / flips | SI-SDRi from Isolated | Other talker leak | Bar |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **mf** run 1 (18.7) | 1/1 | 0 | 4.9 s | 0.75 s / 4.2 % | 0.25 s | 1.25 s / 5.8 % | 0 / 6 | +13.2 dB | -19.8 dB | **GO** |
| **mf** run 2 (7.6) | 1/1 | 0 | 4.8 s | 0.0 s / 0.0 % | 0.0 s | 1.25 s / 5.8 % | 0 / 0 | +13.2 dB | -19.9 dB | **GO** |
| mm (12.1) | 1/1 | 0 | 4.8 s | 0.5 s / 2.7 % | 0.5 s | 1.0 s / 4.7 % | 0 / 2 | +13.1 dB | -19.6 dB | GO |
| ff (9.0) | 1/1 | 0 | 2.8 s | 0.75 s / 3.8 % | 0.25 s | 2.0 s / 9.3 % | 0 / 6 | +13.7 dB | -19.7 dB | GO |
| mm-rt run 1 (8.6) | 1/2 (B's name missed) | 1 dup. of A's name, merged | 3.8 s | 4.25 s / 21.2 % | 3.0 s | 4.0 s / 17.4 % | 0 / 7 | +0.9 dB | -18.2 dB | no-go |
| mm-rt with `am_adam` at 27.6 s (7.1) | 1/2 (B's name missed) | 1 dup., merged | 4.3 s | 3.75 s / 19.5 % | 2.5 s | 3.5 s / 15.9 % | 0 / 8 | +0.5 dB | -18.7 dB | no-go |
| `--overlap`: mf, mm, ff, mm-rt (13-22) | **0/5** | 0 | never | - | - | - | - | - | - | no-go |

Whisper detection latency (name's last audio -> detected, the listener's own number): 0.5-2.4 s (ff 0.54 s at load 9; mf 2.35 s at
load 7.6-18.7, hearing "Aryan" only in the next 2.5 s window). After detection, the clip is prepared and the Enrolled tier fades in
another 2.5-3.5 s. The **wrong-speaker share from the name's end** (5-9 %) is almost all that acquisition window, where `dfn_ll` still
plays both talkers. Once Isolated, the pairs route correctly (0-4.2 %, no stretch over 0.5 s, SI-SDRi +13 dB).
mm-rt's SI-SDRi is low because B is the reference after 28 s and A kept playing.

Why the misses happened, from inline `NameCaller` checks: with B talking over the name (SIR 0 dB plus babble), Whisper writes
"Bye and bye for again" (ff) and misses the name. The T-120 scene itself only just passes, read as "Ariane". Kokoro `am_adam`'s
"Aryan" is unreadable even with A silent ("But the audience over here"). Of seven male voices, `am_echo`, `am_puck` and
`am_fenrir` read as "Aryan"/"Arian" with light noise. `am_echo` in the scene (A's tail ends at 27.8 s, babble at 10 dB) was
still missed in the session.

**A100** (job 435775, `ws://127.0.0.1:18765/ws/session`, `/health` git_rev bc1d8dc-dirty, warm): not measured end to end. The GPU
server reports `name_call.engine: "none"` (faster-whisper is not installed there), so Name Call cannot trigger on it (mf replay:
0/1). On the Mac, Name Call never uses the remote backend: `Session.active_spec` sends only `focus_hq` remote. The job was
not touched. **Profile path** (T-125) and **teammate recordings** (T-126, `$HEARING_FIXTURES_DIR/team/` absent): not on main. Run
`pipeline_eval --team DIR` once the fixtures land.

**Recommendations (with these stand-ins):**
- **T-127 (matcher tuning for same-gender wrong routing): no.** Same-gender pairs route no worse than mixed ones once Isolated:
  mm 2.7 %, ff 3.8 %, mf 0-4.2 %, leak -19.6 to -19.9 dB on all three. Re-check on the teammates' recordings, where two real
  male voices may sit closer than GRID s2 vs a Kokoro voice.
- **T-128 (self-strengthening voice print): no.** Enrollment from one name utterance plus growth to 5 s holds from the first
  Isolated window: no wrong stretch over 0.5 s, 0 re-targets, SI-SDRi +13 dB in every non-re-target scene. Time to Isolated (2.8-4.9 s) is
  dominated by Whisper (0.5-2.4 s) and the clip/fade (2.5-3.5 s), which T-128 would not shorten.
- **What does need work:** the trigger. It got 0/5 names spoken over another talker at SIR 0 dB, and 0/2 re-target calls from a second voice
  in the scene. A missed re-target means the old target keeps playing: 19-21 % wrong, 2.5-3 s stretches. For the demo, have
  the caller say the name while the other person pauses, and keep `name_call: release` on a button.

the margin; 46 windows). They are **not** fitted on real voices.

## T-135: judge output AGC, tse hop pinned after the first Isolated, 2026-10-08
laptop-a (M4 Pro, MPS, local Focus HQ), `scripts/demo_regress.py --modes focus_hq[,natural] --profile judge --no-baseline --no-free`
on demo-mf-easy (60 s), each run under `swarm-lock --exclusive`; load average from `uptime` before -> after each run.
The judge profile levels its output with `hearing.audio.agc` (-20 dBFS, 50/800 ms, held in pauses). A level-tracking gain caps a
whole-clip SI-SDR (offline, SI-SDR(AGC(x), x) = 7.4 dB at these constants), so separation is scored on the output **before** the AGC
(`out_pre_agc.wav`, the tap's copy of the same samples); leak, other-dominant time, DNSMOS and loudness are scored on what plays (`out.wav`).

| run | hop | load | separation SI-SDRi, before output leveling | SI-SDRi of the played (leveled) output | leak dB | other-dominant s | DNSMOS OVRL | first isolated s | hop_changes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| regress-20261008-203420 focus_hq | pinned from Start (`HEARING_JUDGE_ADAPT_HOP=0`) | 4.8 -> 5.3 | +6.5 | +2.98 | -15.0 | 1.4 | 2.65 | 7.4 | 0 |
| regress-20261008-204028 focus_hq | pinned from Start | 4.9 -> 7.6 | +8.5 | +3.73 | -18.7 | 0.8 | 2.71 | 5.9 | 0 |
| regress-20261008-203649 focus_hq | adaptive until Isolated, then pinned (default) | 5.3 -> 4.7 | +8.5 | +3.78 | -18.8 | 0.8 | 2.72 | 5.9 (pinned at 6.0) | 0 |
| regress-20261008-204144 focus_hq | adaptive until Isolated, then pinned | 7.6 -> 5.9 | +8.3 | +3.72 | -18.1 | 1.0 | 2.71 | 5.5 (pinned at 5.6) | 0 |
| regress-20261008-203649 natural | n/a | 5.3 -> 4.7 | +0.1 | -0.75 | -6.7 | 12.4 | 2.51 | - | 0 |

- Hop A/B: at load 4.7-7.6 no run overran, so the adaptive hop never moved before the pin (hop_changes 0 in all four): both behaviours lock in
  5.5-7.4 s and separate +6.5..+8.5 dB, which two runs per arm cannot tell apart. The default keeps the adaptive hop until the first Isolated
  (Q-341) so a heavier load can still widen it before the lock; after the pin no hop change plays as a dip.
- Same branch, attempt 2 (load 7-10): judge profile with the AGC off, focus_hq SI-SDRi +8.91 dB, leak -18.0 dB, other-dominant 0.8 s
  (demo_regress 20261008-200252); the separation before leveling above (+8.3..+8.5 with the default hop) matches it.
- Output loudness (gated 400 ms blocks, no K-weighting, `.swarm-run/loud.py` in the task worktree): input -18.1 dBFS integrated (10 s spans
  -18.8..-17.6). focus_hq output -24.0 / -23.9 dBFS integrated; 10 s spans -25.1..-24.4 after the first, -21.8 / -21.7 in the first 10 s
  (before the lock the ladder plays the louder audio-only/gate mix). natural -23.7 dBFS, spans -24.5..-22.7. Peak 1.000 (soft limiter, no wrap).
- `hearing.tse.headroom` (attempt 1, load 3.0): 56 % headroom (p50 219 / p95 235 ms per 0.5 s hop, MPS).
- `scripts/judge_check.py --flow-only --runs 2 --seconds 20` (load 5.9 -> 6.2, default hop): PASS, lock -> Isolated 5.2 / 5.3 s, pill changes 3
  (look > speak > calibrating > isolated), tse hop_changes 0, jitter underruns 0 at 120 ms. Camera frames stopped at 20 s (attempt 2, 40 s
  replay, load 5.0 -> 10.7): tier enrolled for 100 % of the replay after 21 s.

## T-129: Focus HQ tuning on the A100 with voice profiles, 2026-10-08
(Attempts 1-3 below were blocked; the measured A/B is the **2026-10-09 (attempt 4)** block; the **attempt 5** block after it re-scores everything before the output AGC and adds Mac strict vs standard.)
**A100 A/B not run (attempts 1-3).** The GPU server (job 435775, `ws://127.0.0.1:18765/ws/session`) answers `/health` with `git_rev`
bc1d8dc-dirty (T-124), which predates T-125's voice profiles (603b854): it cannot load a profile, and no
`HEARING_PROFILES_DIR` exists on the Mac or the iLab host yet. T-125 recorded no A100 medians either, so there is no
baseline to beat. No variant (no remote video, hop 0.125, `enrolled_dfn`, `pause_duck_asd`, same_bucket off) became a
default. `scripts/demo_regress.py --voice-profile NAME[:OTHER]` now sends the profile with the mode for the next run.

**Margin from the A-vs-B score gaps** (accuracy only, laptop-a MPS, not load-sensitive). `EnrolledCandidate` (shipped
separator + ECAPA, margin 0) on 0.5 s windows every 0.125 s (the GPU's balanced enrolled window/hop) from 13.5 s to the end of
each clip. The profile is built like T-125's (`profile_clip`, 2.5 s pieces, no separator pass). The target profile is the
target stem's turns ending by 7 s, and the negative is the other stem's turns ending by 13.6 s. These are the same GRID
utterances the clip repeats, so they are **not held out**. A window counts when the target stem is above -40 dBFS.
Truth = the separated stream that correlates best with the target stem. "Held" = gap below the margin, so the window follows
the previous stream. Wrong = the top score is not the truth stream. Script: `.swarm-run/margin_gap.py` (scratch, not in the repo).

| clip | negative | windows | top pick right | gap p10 / p50 / p90 | held at 0.05 (wrong among them) | held at 0.10 (wrong among them) | wrong total / largest wrong gap |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| demo-team-easy (stand-ins s2/s7) | none | 112 | 90.2 % | 0.044 / 0.207 / 0.410 | 13 (5) | 24 (8) | 11 / 0.268 |
| demo-team-easy (stand-ins s2/s7) | profile (cos to target -0.001) | 112 | 90.2 % | 0.096 / 0.378 / 0.619 | 5 (1) | 12 (2) | 11 / 0.508 |
| demo-mf-easy | none | 125 | 91.2 % | 0.056 / 0.291 / 0.474 | 12 (5) | 22 (9) | 11 / 0.141 |
| demo-mf-easy | profile (cos to target 0.045) | 125 | 91.2 % | 0.102 / 0.385 / 0.629 | 7 (4) | 12 (5) | 11 / 0.449 |

What it says: **keep `margin` 0.1** (shipped). Subtracting the negative's cosine roughly doubles the gap (p50 0.21 -> 0.38
on team-easy, 0.29 -> 0.39 on mf-easy). It does not change which stream wins, though: the wrong-pick count is the same
11 per clip. With a negative, margin 0.1 holds about 10 % of windows, 17-42 % of which are wrong picks it does not follow.
Margin 0.05 holds half as many windows and catches fewer wrong picks: 1-4 against 2-5. Without a negative, 0.1 holds 8-9 of
the 11 wrong windows. The wrong picks that remain with a negative have gaps up to 0.45-0.51, so no margin catches them.
Most fall in overlap windows: 8 of 11 on team-easy, 6 of 11 on mf-easy. The correlation truth is weakest there. The same
holds for target-only windows, where the separator splits one voice.
`demo-team-real` (T-126's real two-person take) does not exist yet (`$HEARING_FIXTURES_DIR/team/` absent), so it is not measured.
Wrong-speaker routing (the M12 seconds/% figure from `session_score`) needs the live A/B and was not measured.

Verdict per the acceptance rule: a variant must beat `base`'s medians on both clips on SI-SDRi, leak and DNSMOS first 7 s, with true
dropouts <= 0.2 s, agreement 100 % and no overrun streak > 1.5 s.
- **novideo** (`HEARING_REMOTE_VIDEO=0`): **not adopted.** It frees the remote CPU as predicted: enrolled compute p50 drops from 151-210 to
  91-93 ms on mf-easy, the hop stays at 0.1875 s instead of widening to 0.25 s, there are no overrun streaks (base: 0.7 / 3.6 / 2.6 s),
  DNSMOS first 7 s rises from 1.69 to 2.33 and Isolated comes at 4.3 s instead of 6.0 s. But it loses 0.6 dB SI-SDRi
  (-2.40 vs -1.80) and 1.2 dB leak (-8.1 vs -9.3) on mf-easy. On team-easy it is identical (the clip has no target face).
- **standard** (`--isolation standard` instead of strict / pause_duck_asd): **not adopted.** It wins SI-SDRi and leak on both
  clips (team-easy -1.40 vs -2.69 dB, leak -6.3 vs -3.6; mf-easy -0.81 vs -1.80, leak -12.3 vs -9.3). It loses DNSMOS first 7 s on
  team-easy (2.33 vs 2.53), and on mf-easy it overruns in streaks of 5.5 / 1.6 / 4.4 s (video forwarded). These ran at a laptop load of
  4.9-6.4 against base's 2.8-4.1.
- **novideo + standard** (`t129_ab.sh novideo-standard`): **not measured.** Its 2 team-easy runs (SI-SDRi -2.94 / -2.99, enrolled
  compute p50 394 / 101 ms, overrun streaks 55 / 36 s, Isolated at 41 s / never) ran while another `hearing serve` on the laptop (port 8000,
  not this task's) held two sessions of its own on the same A100 server (`lsof`: two new connections to :18765 at about 17:20 local). They are
  contaminated, so the job was stopped. It is the combination most likely to pass, since it pairs standard's SI-SDRi/leak with novideo's lack of overruns;
  measure it with the A100 to itself.
- **hop 0.125**: this is already the remote's base hop (`config/tiers-remote.yaml`). Live, the adaptive hop widens it to 0.1875-0.25 s
  in every run (delay 825-950 ms). Only novideo r1 on team-easy stayed at 0.125. No variant pins it (`HEARING_JUDGE_ADAPT_HOP=0` is
  a laptop-session env and is not forwarded to the remote).
- **enrolled_dfn post-filter, same_bucket off**: **not measured.** Both are remote-side configs (`tiers-remote-dfn.yaml` / a
  `REMOTE_PIPELINES` side server; the same-bucket guard lives in the team preset), and no such server was up. `config/demo-team.yaml`
  keeps the guard on for aryan/joseph (both male), so same_bucket off does not apply to this team.
- **margin**: keep 0.1 (the attempt-1 gap analysis above). It was not re-measured live.

**GPU or Mac: the Mac.** With the same profiles and clips, the Mac's Focus HQ (enrolled 1.0 s window / 0.5 s hop) beats the best
A100 variant on every quality number: team-easy **+1.55 dB** (pre-AGC +3.53), leak -7.4 dB, DNSMOS first 7 s 2.84, OVRL 2.72,
0.00 s dropouts. The A100's best is -1.40 dB (pre-AGC -0.02), leak -6.3 dB. On mf-easy the Mac gets **+3.10 dB** (pre-AGC +8.18), leak -14.5 dB, OVRL 2.90 and
0.12 s dropouts, against the A100's best of -0.81 dB and -12.3 dB. The Mac also has no overrun streaks and reaches Isolated in 4.3-4.5 s. Its cost is the delay: 1500 ms vs 825-950 ms. This confirms
T-156's A100 team-easy finding (-3.8 dB / -4.7 dB). Hypothesis, not tested here: the A100's 0.5 s enrolled window (T-086's
lowest-delay pick, chosen on GRID mixtures) is too short for the real voices. The bake-off found enrolled SI-SDRi flat from 0.5 to 2 s
windows offline, but live the pre-AGC gap to the Mac's 1.0 s window is 3.5 dB on team-easy and 6.5 dB on mf-easy. The next A100 test is the Mac's
1.0 s / 0.5 s window on the remote (a `REMOTE_PIPELINES` side server; `latency_preset: accuracy` rewrites it to 1.5 s / 0.75 s), run
while no other session is on the server.

  (`standard`) gets -0.02 / +2.70 dB with leak -6.4 / -13.6 dB, and its team-easy dropouts are 0.24 s (over the 0.2 s bar).
  The double AGC explains the post-AGC A100 numbers falling below zero, but not the gap: pre-AGC the gap is still 3.5 dB on team-easy and 5.5 dB on mf-easy.
- **Pre-AGC, the A100 variants against `base`:** novideo is a tie on team-easy and loses 0.15 dB SI-SDRi on mf-easy (+1.51 vs +1.66) while
  gaining DNSMOS first 7 s (2.31 vs 1.95). standard wins SI-SDRi on both clips (-0.02 vs -0.70, +2.70 vs +1.66) and leak (-6.4 vs -4.0,
  -13.6 vs -11.4). It loses DNSMOS first 7 s on team-easy (2.30 vs 2.55) and breaks the dropout bar there (0.24 s). **No A100 default changes.**
- **Isolation on the Mac (the demo backend), standard vs strict:** on demo-mf-easy standard wins clearly: SI-SDRi **+9.06** vs +7.54,
  leak **-19.6** vs -11.9 dB, OVRL 2.94 vs 2.92, the same DNSMOS first 7 s (2.45) and 0.00 s dropouts. On demo-team-easy, the real
  voices, the two tie: +3.54 vs +3.55 dB, leak -10.5 vs -10.8, DNSMOS first 7 s 2.79 for both, OVRL 2.85 vs 2.83, 0.12 s dropouts for both.
  Both runs are bit-identical across the 3 repeats. After the AGC, standard reads +2.20 vs +1.56 dB and leak -11.3 vs -7.4 dB on team-easy. That gap is
  the leveler, not separation. By the acceptance rule (beat on BOTH clips), standard does not become the default: it ties on team-easy.
  **Recommendation (orchestrator's call): `isolation: standard` for the team preset.** It never loses beyond noise (0.01 dB, 0.3 dB leak), and it wins 1.5 dB / 7.7 dB leak on
  mf-easy. Without a camera, strict's pause-duck reads the missing ASD as "target quiet" whenever the window's cosine is below 0.25
  (`hearing/tse/enrolled.py`), and each duck plays only the attenuated room (`hearing/fusion/mixer.py`) instead of the separated stream.
- **A100 1.0 s window / 0.25 s hop:** built, not measured. `config/tiers-remote-w1.yaml` is `config/tiers-remote.yaml` with the
  enrolled tier at `window_s=1.0, hop_s=0.25` (1.25 s delay vs the Mac's 1.5 s). A remote-stage session now leaves the judge AGC to the
  laptop (`Session._agc_on` is false for `hello.client == "remote-stage"`), so a server on this branch's code runs a single AGC.
  It needs a side server on this branch (`REMOTE_PIPELINES='focus_hq=tiers(config="config/tiers-remote-w1.yaml")'` + `ILAB_PROFILES`),
  then `bash scripts/t129_ab.sh w1 w1-standard` against its URL. No new A100 run was taken this attempt: the laptop's port-8000
  `hearing serve` (pid 13641) still held 3 connections to the A100 tunnel (`lsof`, 17:3x local), the contamination that voided novideo-standard in attempt 4.

## T-179: noise-floor-aware output AGC (never up under the floor, max +9 dB), 2026-10-09
**Shipped opt-in, not default** (Q-485 answer: no variant meets every bar, so the default stays the T-169 AGC, +12 dB max;
`HEARING_AGC_FLOOR_RULE=1` on the server turns the floor rule on, and telemetry `agc.floor_rule` makes `/judge` mirror it on RAW).
Offline replay on laptop-a (accuracy only, not load-dependent): `out_pre_agc.wav` of each recorded session run through the
default AGC (`before`: T-169, +12 dB max, a held block may raise the gain) and the floor rule (`after`, `agc.FLOOR_RULE`), same
audio, scored by `session_score` (`.venv/bin/python scripts/agc_replay.py <sessions> --manifest eval/fixtures/demo/demo-mf-hard/manifest.json`).
The AGC is the last stage and has no look-ahead, so the render is exactly what that AGC would have played; no new live or
demo_regress session backs these numbers (a `demo_regress --modes focus_hq` rerun under the judge profile with the env var set is
the required follow-up). Clip: demo-mf-hard (babble SNR 5 dB, cafe on), judge profile, focus_hq, profile grid-s1, demo_regress
runs of 2026-10-09 21:47-21:54 UTC.
Both SI-SDRi columns use **the same delay-aligned crop**: out_pre_agc.wav is shifted by out.wav's measured delays and scored
on out.wav's kept frames (`si_sdri_pre_agc_same_crop_db`; it equals the separately aligned `si_sdri_pre_agc_db` to 0.01 dB on all
six). gap = SI-SDRi minus pre-AGC SI-SDRi. true dropouts = target-active 20 ms frames where out.wav is silent (runs >= 3 frames).
pause lift = out.wav minus out_pre_agc.wav over target-silent 20 ms frames; speech level = out.wav over target-active 20 ms frames,
energy mean / 90th percentile of frame levels; pre-AGC speech = out_pre_agc.wav over the same frames.

|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 20261009T214755Z-1 | before | +3.14 | +7.54 | -4.40 | -13.46 | 0.18 | -1.05 | -24.0 / -20.4 | -19.4 |
| 20261009T214755Z-1 | after | +3.42 | +7.54 | -4.12 | -13.46 | 0.18 | -1.15 | -24.1 / -20.4 | -19.4 |
| 20261009T214908Z-1 | before | +4.33 | +8.90 | -4.57 | -21.51 | 0.18 | -0.55 | -24.1 / -20.4 | -19.4 |

| 20261009T215032Z-1 | before | +1.54 | +4.55 | -3.01 | -11.10 | 0.12 | -3.06 | -24.3 / -20.4 | -19.2 |
| 20261009T215032Z-1 | after | +1.77 | +4.55 | -2.78 | -11.10 | 0.12 | -3.13 | -24.4 / -20.4 | -19.2 |
| 20261009T215146Z-1 | before | +4.39 | +9.06 | -4.67 | -21.51 | 0.18 | -0.55 | -24.1 / -20.4 | -19.4 |
| 20261009T215146Z-1 | after | +4.78 | +9.06 | -4.28 | -21.51 | 0.18 | -0.87 | -24.1 / -20.4 | -19.4 |
| 20261009T215259Z-1 | before | +3.41 | +8.05 | -4.64 | -13.88 | 0.12 | -0.95 | -24.0 / -20.4 | -19.4 |
| 20261009T215259Z-1 | after | +3.70 | +8.05 | -4.35 | -13.88 | 0.12 | -1.05 | -24.1 / -20.4 | -19.4 |
| 20261009T215412Z-1 | before | +4.47 | +9.26 | -4.79 | -21.51 | 0.12 | -0.55 | -24.1 / -20.4 | -19.4 |
| 20261009T215412Z-1 | after | +4.86 | +9.26 | -4.40 | -21.51 | 0.12 | -0.87 | -24.1 / -20.4 | -19.4 |

Bars (floor rule, `after`):

| bar | result | evidence |
|---|---|---|
| pause lift <= +3 dB | **PASS** | -0.87 to -3.13 dB (before -0.55 to -3.06) |
| leak no worse than before | **PASS** | identical to 0.01 dB on all six |
| true dropouts | no change | 0.12-0.18 s, same before and after |
| SI-SDRi within 0.5 dB of pre-AGC | **FAIL** | gap -2.78 to -4.40 dB (before -3.01 to -4.79); +0.2-0.4 dB better than the default, still far from 0.5 |
| speech within +-3 dB of -20 dBFS | **FAIL** by energy mean (-24.1 to -24.4 dBFS); PASS by p90 (-20.4 dBFS) | same as before (-24.0 to -24.3 / -20.4) |

Where the speech-level offset comes from: not the measure alone and not the 9 dB cap, but the AGC itself. The pre-AGC speech is already
at -19.2/-19.4 dBFS (energy mean), so the AGC *cuts* it by about 5 dB: its envelope (50 ms attack, 800 ms release, blocks 12 dB under
it held) follows the loud syllables and levels those to -20 dBFS (p90 -20.4), which leaves the energy mean 4 dB lower. The same render
with the cap lifted to +30 dB gives -23.9 dBFS (20261009T214755Z-1), so the cap plays no part; the default T-169/T-135 AGC
shows the same -24 dBFS. It is a limitation of the T-135 leveling design (logged in `docs/debt/T-179.md`), not something the floor rule
introduced or can fix. The SI-SDRi gap has the same cause: the speech-following gain varies within utterances; with the pause relax off
(gap 4.2 dB) or a 3 s release (3.2-3.9 dB) it does not close (attempt 1 variants).

Live mic sessions (no stems, so "quiet" = 20 ms frames of out_pre_agc.wav 25 dB under its 95th percentile, a stem-free
stand-in for target-silent; loud = frames within 10 dB of it), same offline before/after:

| session | length | quiet share | pause lift before -> after dB | loud-frame level before -> after dBFS |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 20261009T203531Z-2 | 369 s | 27 % | +2.0 -> +1.6 | -22.0 -> -22.1 |
| 20261009T211905Z-8 | 231 s | 16 % | +0.7 -> +0.7 | -21.9 -> -21.9 |
| 20261009T211806Z-7 | 49 s | 50 % | +2.9 -> +1.3 | -25.4 -> -27.4 |
| 20261009T204548Z-4 | 10 s | 26 % | +5.9 -> +5.0 | -21.4 -> -21.6 |

Verdict: the floor rule meets pause lift (<= +3 dB on all six cafe-on replays, -0.9 to -3.1 dB; 3 of 4 live sessions, the 10 s
one at +5.0 dB is shorter than the noise-floor window needs to settle) and leak (unchanged). **FAIL: SI-SDRi within 0.5 dB of pre-AGC**
(gap 2.8-4.4 dB) **and FAIL: speech level within +-3 dB of -20 dBFS** (-24 dBFS energy mean; -20.4 dBFS p90); both fail equally with
the default AGC, so they are limitations of the leveling, not of this rule. Per Q-485 the defaults are unchanged and the rule is opt-in
pending the orchestrator's review.

## T-195: Enhanced Replay research — does longer context improve the same raw take? (2026-10-09)

**REPLAY / ENHANCED research renders, not live output.** Nothing here goes on stage without T-171 and a rehearsal.

Run `enhanced_20261009T215658Z`: `swarm-lock --exclusive -- .venv/bin/python -m eval.enhanced_replay` on `Aryans-MacBook-Pro.local` (M4 Pro, torch `mps`), git `080e197`, load average 7.73 when queued for the lock / 2.77 at the end (the lock waited 10 s for two verifies to finish). DNSMOS by the ONNX P.835 scorer. Manifests (input path + sha256, config, context, device, render s), `results.json` and level-matched (-26 dBFS active RMS) A/B wavs are in `<main>/results/enhanced_20261009T215658Z/<take>/` (gitignored): `raw.wav`, `A_LIVE.wav` (sessions: what was heard, delay-aligned) or `A_LIVE_window1s.wav` (T-141: the live window), and `B_REPLAY_ENHANCED_ctx<1|5|15|full>[_dfn].wav`.

Setup: the enrolled candidate (`tse:enrolled` defaults, standard isolation; `aryan` profile as target, `joseph` as the known negative from `$HEARING_FIXTURES_DIR/profiles`) through `SlidingWindowExtractor` at window = context, hop = window / 2 (`1` = the live balanced window 1.0 s / 0.5 s, `full` = one window over the whole take), each with and without the `dfn` post-filter of `enrolled_dfn` (`tse>dfn>mixer`, mixer at s = 1). T-141 scenes have stems: leak dB = least-squares gain of the other talker's stem relative to the target's in the overlap section (lower = better), SI-SDRi vs the clean target, true dropouts = 0.25 s target-active windows (stem within 20 dB of its speech level) where the output is > 20 dB below its own active level. Sessions (`results/sessions/<stamp>/in.wav`, 45 s excerpt where the live ladder played enrolled / focus_hq most; `full` = the whole excerpt) have no stems: leak / SI-SDRi n/a, target-active time comes from the ECAPA proxy, so their dropout counts are rough (the raw input itself scores 12 runs on 191504Z-2). `wrong-voice %` = share of voiced 1.5 s output windows whose ECAPA embedding is closer to `joseph` than to `aryan` (a proxy; it reads 22-49 % on the raw rooms). `render s` = wall clock of the TSE pass (+ dfn); the first render of the run (easy, ctx 1, 30.9 s) includes MPS warm-up, the same render on the byte-identical `same` scene took 13.6 s.

`t141:demo-team-same` is omitted: its `stems/mix.wav` is byte-identical to `demo-team-easy` (sha1 `271d7237`), and its rows reproduced easy's to the last digit. Session crops: 191504Z-2 0-45 s (live mostly audio-only there), 203531Z-2 157-202 s (live lag 880 ms, A100 remote), 211905Z-8 67-112 s (live lag 940 ms, A100 remote).

**Verdict.** On the two reference scenes, extra context helps the target: SI-SDRi 6.3 → 9.1 → 11.5 → 10.9 dB (easy, 1 / 5 / 15 s / full) and 4.2 → 9.0 → 9.4 → 11.0 dB (hard), leak -16.6 → -40.8 dB at 15 s (easy) and -18.8 → -25.4 dB at full (hard); leak is not monotonic (hard at 15 s, -17.7 dB, is worse than at 5 s). **On the real session takes there is no measured win and full-take context is worse**: wrong-voice proxy 29 % / 20 % at full vs 3 % / 8 % at the 1 s window (203531Z-2 / 191504Z-2), DNSMOS OVRL -0.23 to +0.14 against the 1 s window. The dfn post-filter moves DNSMOS BAK by -0.04 to +0.43 and cuts leak by 0.9-5.3 dB on the reference scenes, at a cost of up to 1.1 dB SI-SDRi. So: **no context wins on real takes; stop before staging Enhanced Replay.** 15 s is the only candidate worth a blind headphone A/B (`B_REPLAY_ENHANCED_ctx15_dfn.wav` vs `A_LIVE*.wav`), and full-take must not be used (speaker flips on 2 of 3 real takes).

## T-178: Enrolled contrast margin sweep on the team scenes, pair cosine, margin refit (2026-10-09)
**Adoption rule (from the task):** adopt a contrast value in `config/demo-team.yaml` only if wrong_pct falls **and** SI-SDRi drops <= 1 dB.
**Verdict: not adopted; the default stays `contrast` 0.** The offline sweep below meets the rule at 0.3. The session-level confirmation
(`demo_regress --profile team`) did not run: in its only run the enrolled tier never played. Offline output is not what a listener hears
(see the caveat), so a win measured only offline does not count.

**Pair cosine (the SAME_VOICE = 0.7 trap):** cos(aryan, joseph) = **0.018** with the current profiles
(`$HEARING_FIXTURES_DIR/profiles`: aryan 50.0 s, joseph 13.95 s, loaded as `Session.load_voice_profile` does). That is far below 0.7,
so **the negative is active** and the pick is scored by the closed-set LDA (`scoring: lda` in every window). Live sessions now show it in
telemetry (`tse.pair_cos`, `tse.negative_active`, `tse.contrast`), and the `/judge` drawer prints it next to the confidence.

**Offline sweep** (`python -m eval.contrast_sweep --profiles <generated>/profiles --scene <generated>/t141/aryan/demo-team-easy --scene .../demo-team-hard`,
laptop-a M4 Pro, MPS, real weights, deterministic: the contrast-0 rows reproduced exactly in a second run). `EnrolledCandidate` runs over 1.0 s windows
every 0.5 s with aryan as target and joseph as the negative. Metrics:
- wrong_pct: share of target-active windows whose ungated output correlates more with joseph's stem;
- longest_wrong_s: the longest run of such windows;
- gated: target-active windows the floor silenced;
- target-absent dB: output minus input level over the 6 hops where Aryan is silent and Joseph talks;
- SI-SDRi and leak: on the stitched TSE stream alone (each window's last hop, no mixer residual).
`-same` is left out: its mix is identical to `-easy` (Q-474). Results: `eval/results/contrast_sweep_20261009-200458.json`.

| Scene | contrast | wrong_pct | longest_wrong_s | gated | target-absent out vs in | SI-SDRi (TSE stream) | leak | Run id |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| demo-team-easy | 0 | 11.3 % | 1.0 | 1.9 % | -15.2 dB | +1.17 dB | -19.0 dB | `contrast_sweep_20261009-200458` |
| demo-team-easy | 0.1 | 7.5 % | 1.0 | 1.9 % | -41.3 dB | +1.99 dB | -20.5 dB | `contrast_sweep_20261009-200458` |
| demo-team-easy | 0.2 | 5.7 % | 0.5 | 5.7 % | -41.3 dB | +2.56 dB | -22.6 dB | `contrast_sweep_20261009-200458` |
| demo-team-easy | 0.3 | 3.8 % | 0.5 | 13.2 % | -41.3 dB | +2.51 dB | -36.9 dB | `contrast_sweep_20261009-200458` |
| demo-team-hard | 0 | 11.3 % | 1.5 | 1.9 % | -37.7 dB | -1.27 dB | -21.6 dB | `contrast_sweep_20261009-200458` |
| demo-team-hard | 0.1 | 11.3 % | 1.5 | 1.9 % | -39.8 dB | +0.39 dB | -27.4 dB | `contrast_sweep_20261009-200458` |
| demo-team-hard | 0.2 | 9.4 % | 1.0 | 3.8 % | -53.9 dB | +2.45 dB | -20.3 dB | `contrast_sweep_20261009-200458` |
| demo-team-hard | 0.3 | 5.7 % | 0.5 | 11.3 % | -67.8 dB | +3.94 dB | -21.5 dB | `contrast_sweep_20261009-200458` |

Caveat: offline, a gated window is silence. In a session, a gated window has confidence 0, so the T-134 mixer floor plays the **mix**
ducked by only 6 dB (both voices), not silence. Offline SI-SDRi and target-absent dB therefore flatter contrast. wrong_pct and
gated share carry over: at 0.3, 11-13 % of the target's windows would drop to the ducked mix.

**Session level (not measured).** The batch `swarm-lock --exclusive -- bash .swarm-run/batch.sh` ran `demo_regress.py --modes focus_hq --seconds 30 --no-free
--no-baseline --profile team --voice-profile aryan:joseph --clip t141/aryan/<scene>` with `HEARING_ENROLLED_CONTRAST` set per row, on the local backend
(no `HEARING_REMOTE_URL`). Its first row (contrast 0, demo-team-easy, load 8.78 before the run, `demo_regress_20261009-200458`) never played
the enrolled tier. Profiles were loaded (`enrollment.source: profile`), but the tier read `overrun`. Every other tier was ineligible
(`gate`: no camera under the audio-only preset; `focus_hq`: needs CUDA), so the session played `audio-only` throughout.
Result: SI-SDRi +0.09 dB, leak -5.3 dB, delay 13 ms. This task's offline margin-fit process overlapped that row's first minute, so the overrun
is partly self-inflicted; the batch was stopped there. `pipeline_eval --profile --team` does not read the T-141 `demo_scene`
layout (it wants `mix.wav` + `profile_target.wav`/`profile_other.wav` + `names` at the dir root), so it was not run either.
To finish: rerun the batch alone on an idle Mac or on the A100 (`HEARING_REMOTE_URL`; the env must reach the remote server). Adopt with
`enrolled_contrast: <value>` in `config/demo-team.yaml` (wired: `team.apply_env` → `HEARING_ENROLLED_CONTRAST`) only if the rule holds there.

**Confidence-margin refit on the real voices** (the same offline windows at contrast 0; `fit_margin` from `eval/confidence_fit.py`, smallest LDA margin
with >= 97 % / 95 % of target-active picks right above it): demo-team-easy **0.314 / 0.314**, demo-team-hard **0.413 / 0.359** (`contrast_sweep_20261009-201221`; hard also reproduced wrong_pct 11.3 %)
(`CONF_MARGIN_LDA` today is 0.58, fitted on the GRID stand-in). Only 53 active windows per scene, both from one recorded pair, so the constant
is not changed here. A lower `CONF_MARGIN_LDA` ducks the residual deeper on more windows, which also raises leak when a pick is confidently wrong
(11 % of picks are wrong at contrast 0).

## T-180: gated / unsure windows: mixer-floor knobs K2, K3, K5 and the P2 post-filter (2026-10-09)
**Adoption rule (from the task):** "Adopt a knob as the team-preset default only if wrong-speaker % does not rise, leak improves >= 3 dB
and true dropouts rise <= 0.2 s; otherwise document." **Verdict: none adopted; `config/demo-team.yaml` is unchanged.** Every knob ships
off by default behind an env var:

| Knob | Env | Effect |
|---|---|---|
| K2 | `HEARING_GATED_FLOOR_DB=20` | a window at confidence exactly 0 (gated) ducks the residual 20 dB, not 6 (`mixer.confidence_floor_db`) |
| K3 | `HEARING_UNSURE_DB=12` | the confidence-0 end of the T-134 line is 12 dB, not 6 (12 + c * 28) |
| K5 | `HEARING_GATED_PLAY=stream` | a two-profile window gated by contrast/floor plays its best stream (confidence 0), not silence (`EnrolledCandidate.extract`) |
| P2 | `HEARING_TSE_POSTFILTER=1` | every enrolled `tse(...)>mixer` in a ladder becomes `tse(...)>dfn>mixer` (`latency_preset.apply_postfilter`) |

**Session A/B (planned, not usable on this host).** `demo_regress.py --modes focus_hq --seconds 30 --no-free --no-baseline --profile team
--voice-profile aryan:joseph --isolation {strict,standard} --pre-agc --clip t141/aryan/demo-team-easy` (laptop-a M4 Pro, MPS, local backend,
no video, load 3.3-4.7 before the runs). Both rows never played the enrolled tier: it read `overrun` (compute p50 0.7-3.9 s per 0.5 s hop,
hop widened to 1.0 s) and the ladder stayed on `audio-only` for the whole 30 s clip, so strict and standard scored the same:
SI-SDRi +1.04 dB, leak -11.0 dB, true dropouts 0.08 s, DNSMOS 2.85, delay 13 ms (`regress-20261009-212252`, `regress-20261009-212345`;
the first row overlapped ~30 s of an orphaned server from this task's own aborted batch). A row that never plays enrolled cannot show a mixer-floor
effect, so the batch was stopped there (same failure as T-178's session rows). The `swarm-lock --exclusive` slot was never granted either:
its two "verify" holders were the long-lived `swarm run`/`swarm serve` PIDs (stale slot entries), so the batch ran with `--wait 10`.

**Offline A/B (what is measured).** `python -m eval.floor_ab --profiles <generated>/profiles --scene <generated>/t141/aryan/demo-team-easy
--scene .../demo-team-hard` (laptop-a, MPS, real ECAPA + separator, DNSMOS ONNX). The real `EnrolledCandidate` (aryan target, joseph negative,
contrast 0, closed-set LDA) runs 1.0 s windows every 0.5 s; each hop is then mixed as the session mixer would at slider 100:
`out = s + 10^(-floor/20) * (mix - s)` (no gain ramp, pre-AGC). Wrong-speaker % / longest wrong: `pipeline_eval.routing` (0.25 s windows,
target intended throughout). True dropouts: target-active 0.5 s hops whose target gain in the output is < -10 dB. Strict offline only adds
the pause duck (`pause_floor 0.25`; no ASD offline, so `pause_p` never fires), which did not trigger on these scenes: strict = standard here.
`-same` is left out (its mix is identical to `-easy`, Q-474). P2 is not in the offline A/B: it changes the extracted stream (DFN at 48 kHz
on the tse output) and its cost is the live question (T-110: the post-filter pushed the remote enrolled tier into overrun); not measured here.

| demo-team-hard | standard | +0.16 dB | -16.5 dB | 8.00 s | 2.58 | 6.2 % | 0.75 s | 3/60 | `floor_ab_20261009-212547` |

| demo-team-hard | standard+conf0 | +0.09 dB | -15.2 dB | 10.50 s | 2.64 | 8.3 % | 0.75 s | 3/60 | `floor_ab_20261009-212547` |
| demo-team-hard | standard+K2 | +0.27 dB | -18.5 dB | 8.50 s | 2.67 | 6.2 % | 0.75 s | 3/60 | `floor_ab_20261009-212547` |
| demo-team-hard | standard+K3 | -0.18 dB | -18.2 dB | 10.00 s | 2.63 | 7.3 % | 0.75 s | 3/60 | `floor_ab_20261009-212547` |
| demo-team-hard | standard+K5 | +0.14 dB | -16.5 dB | 8.00 s | 2.54 | 6.2 % | 0.75 s | 3/60 | `floor_ab_20261009-212547` |
| demo-team-hard | standard+K2+K5 | +0.25 dB | -18.5 dB | 8.50 s | 2.59 | 6.2 % | 0.75 s | 3/60 | `floor_ab_20261009-212547` |

What it says. (1) **Gating is rare with aryan as target at contrast 0** (1/60 hops on easy, 3/60 on hard), so K2 and K5 only act on a few hops.
K2 improves leak by 0.4 / 2.0 dB (easy / hard) but adds 0.5 s of true dropouts on both scenes. That fails the rule: leak must improve by
>= 3 dB with dropouts up <= 0.2 s. K5 changes nothing measurable (DNSMOS -0.04 on hard). (2) **K3 fails:** wrong-speaker rises
(5.2 -> 6.2 %, 6.2 -> 7.3 %), leak improves only 0.9 / 1.7 dB, and dropouts rise 1.0 / 2.0 s. (3) **The T-134 confidence floor beats
`HEARING_TSE_CONFIDENCE=0`** on both scenes (wrong-speaker 5.2 vs 8.3 % and 6.2 vs 8.3 %; leak 2.5 / 1.3 dB better), so it stays on.
(4) The live symptom from the task (median output +2.3 dB above input on the A100 session) is not reproduced by this offline mixing. It
comes from the session ladder (audio-only/overrun) and the output AGC (T-179's area), not from these few gated hops. The joseph-as-target
case (13-29 of 85 windows wrongly gated, `confidence_fit`) was not run in this task, and that is where K2/K5 would act most. The offline
"true dropouts" (a hop's target gain < -10 dB) count wrong picks, so they are larger than `demo_regress`'s and only comparable within this table.
To finish: rerun `eval.floor_ab` with `--target joseph --other aryan` on `t141/joseph/*`, and the session rows on an idle Mac or the A100 once
the enrolled tier plays there.

## Demo path health (idle Mac, Oct 10)
T-284. Question: on a quiet Mac, does the stage config (`config/demo-team.yaml`: team preset, standard isolation, contrast 0, saved
profiles aryan/joseph, local backend, audio only) keep the enrolled tier up in real time? **Answer: yes. In every row the enrolled tier ran
58 windows with 0 overruns and 0 late windows, at a compute p95 of 249-257 ms per 0.5 s hop (RTF 0.48).** Verdict: `docs/decisions/T-DEMO-HEALTH.md`.

**How it ran.** One batch, laptop-a (`Aryans-MacBook-Pro.local`, Apple M4 Pro, MPS, no `HEARING_REMOTE_URL`), git `fa62acc`, 2026-10-10 00:14-00:17 local:
`swarm-lock --exclusive --max-load 4 --wait 1200 -- bash .swarm-run/batch.sh`. The lock waited 52 s for two verify runs to finish, then started
at a 1-min load of 3.86. Each row is one `demo_regress` run with its own server, run one after another:

```
HEARING_PROFILES_DIR=$HEARING_FIXTURES_DIR/profiles HEARING_REGRESS_NO_BASELINE=1 \
  .venv/bin/python scripts/demo_regress.py --modes focus_hq --seconds 30 --no-free --no-baseline \
  --profile team --isolation standard --voice-profile joseph:aryan --clip $HEARING_FIXTURES_DIR/t141/joseph/demo-team-easy
#   ... --voice-profile aryan:joseph --clip $HEARING_FIXTURES_DIR/t141/aryan/demo-team-easy
#   ... --voice-profile joseph:aryan --clip $HEARING_FIXTURES_DIR/t141/joseph/demo-team-hard
#   ... --voice-profile aryan:joseph --clip $HEARING_FIXTURES_DIR/t141/aryan/demo-team-hard
```

**Clips.** The rows use the real-voice T-141 scenes `$HEARING_FIXTURES_DIR/t141/<target>/demo-team-{easy,hard}` (aryan and joseph
recordings, the same scenes T-178/T-180 used). The task named `eval/fixtures/demo/demo-team-{easy,hard}`, but those clips are GRID stand-ins
(manifest: "stand-in GRID grid-s2 (target, left) vs grid-s7"). The aryan/joseph profiles would not match those voices, so their quality numbers
would mean nothing. `focus_hq` mode runs the ladder `enrolled > gate > audio-only`. `gate` is ineligible because the preset is audio only, and `focus_hq` is ineligible because it needs CUDA.

**Columns.** `load` = `uptime` 1/5/15-min load average just before the row. `enrolled share` = the share of telemetry windows whose tier was
`enrolled` (`demo_regress.enrolled_share`). `deadline misses` = the enrolled health's `overruns` + `late` out of `windows`, read at the session's last tick.
`p95 compute` = enrolled health `compute_p95_ms` per window (1.0 s window, 0.5 s hop). SI-SDRi is on `out.wav` (after the output AGC), with the
pre-AGC separation SI-SDRi in brackets. `leak` = the other talker's level in the output vs the input. `wrong-speaker` =
`other_dominant_s` / scored seconds (28.4 s), from `session_score` (frames where the other talker's part beats the target's, makes up >= 10 % of the
output and is ducked < 10 dB). It includes the 3.9 s cold start. Session JSON: `$HEARING_RESULTS_DIR/demo_regress_<stamp>.json`.
Per-row extraction: `$HEARING_RESULTS_DIR/T-284_demo_health_20261010-0014.jsonl`.

Every row: profile enrollment (`enrollment.source: profile`), `negative_active` true, pair cosine 0.018, contrast 0.0, hop 0.5 s (never widened),
delay 1500 ms, 1 tier switch (`audio-only` -> `enrolled`), and 0 switches back.

**Where the missing 13 % goes.** The session start has 31 ticks (0.10-3.24 s) with the enrolled tier `loading`. Then 5 ticks have it eligible but not yet
faded in, and from 3.83-3.87 s every tick is `enrolled` to the end of the clip. On a 30 s clip that cold start alone costs 13 % of windows.
After it, the share is 100 %, so it is not an overrun.

**Extras not run.** The task allowed the contrast 0.2 and `HEARING_AGC_FLOOR_RULE=1` rows only if the enrolled tier played >= 90 % of windows.
The batch applied that gate literally (`enrolled share` over the whole session). Every row read 87 %, so the batch skipped them. The shortfall is the cold start,
not real-time trouble, so a follow-up can run those two rows with no other change: the same command plus `HEARING_ENROLLED_CONTRAST=0.2`, or
`HEARING_AGC_FLOOR_RULE=1`, on `t141/aryan/demo-team-hard`.
T-309 ran them on both hard scenes (the four `(T-309)` rows in the table above; follow-up below).

**Follow-up: contrast 0.2 and the AGC floor rule (T-309).** Same command and clips as the T-284 hard rows, one knob per row set in the
environment (`HEARING_ENROLLED_CONTRAST=0.2` or `HEARING_AGC_FLOOR_RULE=1`); no default or config changed. One batch on laptop-a, git `33a2c28`,
2026-10-10 00:58-01:01 local: `swarm-lock --exclusive --max-load 4 --wait 1200 -- bash .swarm-run/batch.sh` (waited 70 s for a verify, then
started at a 1-min load of 3.89). Telemetry confirms each knob reached the server: enrolled health `contrast` 0.2 in the contrast rows,
`agc.floor_rule` true in the floor-rule rows. Per-row extraction: `$HEARING_RESULTS_DIR/T-309_knobs_20261010-0057.jsonl` (log `.log` next to it).
Each row after the cold start: enrolled share 100 %, 0 tier switches back, compute p95 251-266 ms per 0.5 s hop.

|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| contrast 0.2 | aryan | 9.2 % -> 11.3 % | -9.7 -> -8.8 dB | +1.04 -> +3.19 dB | +0.18 -> +1.07 dB | 0 -> **1** / 58 (1 late) |
| contrast 0.2 | joseph | 19.7 % -> 22.5 % | -6.5 -> -6.0 dB | +2.95 -> +0.99 dB | +0.43 -> -0.59 dB | 0 -> 0 / 58 |

Verdict: **neither knob beats the baseline on both targets.** Contrast 0.2 raises wrong-speaker time and leak on both targets, costs joseph
2 dB of pre-AGC separation (aryan gains 2.2 dB), and added one late window on the aryan row. The AGC floor rule leaves separation, leak and
wrong-speaker time unchanged (it acts after separation) and adds only +0.1-0.2 dB post-AGC SI-SDRi with no new misses. Keep contrast 0 for the demo; the floor rule is neutral.

Read with care: one 30 s run per row. The floor-rule rows reproduce the T-284 pre-AGC SI-SDRi, leak and wrong-speaker values exactly, so
the separation output repeats run to run on these clips and the contrast differences are the knob, not noise. The single late window
(compute p95 250.5 ms, no overrun) is one event in one run and may not repeat.

**Compared with T-178/T-180.** Those session rows read `overrun` (T-180: compute p50 0.7-3.9 s per 0.5 s hop) at load 3.3-8.8. The load-average
range overlaps this run's, so a low load average is not enough to tell a healthy run. What differed: those runs overlapped other heavy
processes (T-178's own offline fit, an orphaned server in T-180), and T-180's exclusive slot was never granted. Here `swarm-lock --exclusive`
held the machine, and compute p95 sat at ~255 ms, half the 500 ms hop budget.

Quality (not the question here, for the record): pre-AGC separation +1.0 to +4.4 dB, and +0.2 to +1.8 dB after the output AGC. Wrong-speaker
time is 9.2 % with aryan as target and 14.8-19.7 % with joseph as target, inside T-141's 6-25 % range.

## T-324: asymmetric hysteretic mixer floor + one-hop lookahead, knob `HEARING_MIXER_HYSTERESIS` (2026-10-10)
Research T-286 pick 1. Knob on: the `tse` stage hands the mixer the lowest confidence over the next 0.5 s hop of output
(`tse_confidence_ahead`, from its 1.5 s output delay); the floor target is `confidence_floor_db(min(conf, ahead))`, followed per sample in dB
with a 50 ms attack (more ducking) and a 160 ms release (back toward the mix; 3 tau fits inside the hop of lookahead). Knob unset = the T-134
mixer bit for bit (`tests/test_mixer_hysteresis.py`). **Verdict: not adopted; `config/demo-team.yaml` unchanged.** Per the
orchestrator (Q-756), the knob, its tests and the `eval/floor_ab.py` rows used below were reverted before merge. The patch lives on branch
`task/T-324` (commits `809ef67c`, `edabe6e1`); the tables are the record.
Wrong-speaker rises on 2/4 offline rows and 3/4 session rows. Decision note: `docs/decisions/T-324-mixer-hysteresis.md`.

**Offline A/B** (`eval.floor_ab --combos mixer,mixer+hyst[,mixer+hyst-noahead]`, laptop-a M4 Pro, MPS, real `EnrolledCandidate`, standard
isolation, 1.0 s windows every 0.5 s). Each row runs the real `Mixer` stage in 10 ms chunks at slider 100, pre-AGC: knob off (`mixer`, the
T-134 floor with its 30 ms ramp; it reproduces T-180's `standard` wrong-speaker/leak), knob on (`mixer+hyst`), and a diagnostic, hysteresis
without the lookahead hint (`-noahead`). `swing` = total variation of the applied floor (dB, 10 ms steps) per second. `clicks` = samples whose
applied gain steps by > 0.01. `dropouts` = target-active 0.5 s hops with target gain < -10 dB (T-180 definition). aryan rows: `--target aryan --other
joseph` on `t141/aryan/*`; joseph rows: `--target joseph --other aryan` on `t141/joseph/*`.

|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
