**Session A/B** (the T-284 command, one `swarm-lock --exclusive --max-load 4 --wait 1500 -- bash .swarm-run/batch.sh` call; lock waited 69 s for two
verifies, then ran 01:53-01:58 local; laptop-a, MPS, local backend, audio only, git `edabe6e`). Each row:
`HEARING_PROFILES_DIR=$HEARING_FIXTURES_DIR/profiles HEARING_REGRESS_NO_BASELINE=1 [HEARING_MIXER_HYSTERESIS=1] .venv/bin/python scripts/demo_regress.py
--modes focus_hq --seconds 30 --no-free --no-baseline --profile team --isolation standard --voice-profile <t>:<o> --clip $HEARING_FIXTURES_DIR/t141/<t>/demo-team-<scene>`.
Every row exited 0 with enrolled delay 1500 ms. Columns come from `python -m eval.floor_ab --session-json <demo_regress json>`. wrong-speaker = `other_dominant_s` / 28.4 s
(includes the ~3.9 s cold start). leak / true dropouts / pre-AGC SI-SDRi are from `session_score`. deadline misses = enrolled `overruns + late` / windows at
the last tick. swing = total variation per second of (out_pre_agc - delay-aligned input) level in 50 ms frames on the enrolled stretch. Separation
moves this too, so it reads higher than the offline floor swing and only compares within this table. clicks = 10 ms frames whose high-band energy is >= 10 dB above
both the aligned input's and the output's own +-50 ms median. Extraction: `$HEARING_RESULTS_DIR/T-324_session_rows_20261010-0153.jsonl`, log `T-324_batch_20261010-0153.log`.

| Scene | target:other | knob | load (1/5/15) | wrong-speaker | leak | true dropouts | swing dB/s | clicks | deadline misses | p95 compute / window | SI-SDRi pre-AGC | Run |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| demo-team-easy | joseph:aryan | off | 3.92 / 4.83 / 4.60 | 14.8 % | -8.1 dB | 0.00 s | 60.7 | 0 | 1 / 58 | 363.0 ms | +4.37 dB | `demo_regress_20261010-015313` |
| demo-team-easy | joseph:aryan | on | 3.79 / 4.68 / 4.55 | **16.9 %** | -7.3 dB | 0.00 s | 54.4 | 0 | 1 / 58 | 362.6 ms | +4.47 dB | `demo_regress_20261010-015354` |
| demo-team-easy | aryan:joseph | off | 4.48 / 4.72 / 4.57 | 9.2 % | -10.3 dB | 0.06 s | 85.4 | 0 | 1 / 58 | 363.1 ms | +3.47 dB | `demo_regress_20261010-015435` |
| demo-team-easy | aryan:joseph | on | 3.55 / 4.46 / 4.48 | **10.6 %** | -9.6 dB | 0.06 s | 78.4 | 0 | 1 / 58 | 364.7 ms | +3.36 dB | `demo_regress_20261010-015515` |
| demo-team-hard | joseph:aryan | off | 3.60 / 4.35 / 4.44 | 19.7 % | -6.5 dB | 0.00 s | 49.3 | 0 | 1 / 58 | 364.9 ms | +2.97 dB | `demo_regress_20261010-015556` |
| demo-team-hard | joseph:aryan | on | 3.39 / 4.20 / 4.38 | 19.7 % | -6.5 dB | 0.00 s | 42.6 | 0 | 1 / 58 | 368.2 ms | +2.95 dB | `demo_regress_20261010-015637` |
| demo-team-hard | aryan:joseph | off | 2.80 / 3.95 / 4.28 | 9.2 % | -9.7 dB | 0.10 s | 81.0 | 0 | 0 / 58 | 363.5 ms | +1.04 dB | `demo_regress_20261010-015717` |
| demo-team-hard | aryan:joseph | on | 2.93 / 3.83 / 4.22 | **11.3 %** | -9.3 dB | 0.10 s | 73.5 | 0 | **1 / 58** | 363.5 ms | +1.16 dB | `demo_regress_20261010-015757` |

**Against the T-180 bar.** (1) Wrong-speaker must not rise on any row: it rises on 2/4 offline rows (aryan: +1.0 / +3.2 points) and 3/4 session rows
(+2.1, +1.4, +2.1 points). **Fail.** (2) Leak >= 3 dB better or swing clearly down with no new clicks: leak is 0.5-1.6 dB worse offline on all four rows
and 0.0-0.8 dB worse in the sessions. Fail on leak. Swing falls 24-53 % offline and 8-14 % in the sessions (that column includes separation movement), with 0 clicks
everywhere. Swing passes. (3) Dropouts +<= 0.2 s: offline -0.5 to -1.0 s, sessions +0.00. Pass. (4) 0 new deadline misses: one row went 0 -> 1 of 58. Three
knob-off rows also had 1/58, compute p95 ~363 ms on both arms (vs ~255 ms in T-284), and the knob adds one min over 8000 samples per 10 ms chunk.
So the miss is load noise, not the knob, but by the letter it is a new miss.

**Why wrong-speaker rises.** At slider 100 the doubt floor (6 dB) is near the mix, and in this pair the other talker is often the louder one there.
The lookahead plays more of the mix around doubtful windows (up to 0.5 s earlier), so more 0.25 s windows are other-dominant. The diagnostic row
shows the split. Hysteresis alone does not raise wrong-speaker (equal on aryan, -2.2 / -1.1 points on joseph) and improves leak 0.4-1.3 dB. But
it adds 0.5-2.0 s of dropouts and leaves the swing unchanged (-1 to -4 %), so it fails the bar too. The swing cut comes from the lookahead
merging flips, and the lookahead is also what costs wrong-speaker. A follow-up that wants the swing cut without that cost would need the lookahead to
move toward the mix only when the doubtful window's best stream is not the louder talker. That is not measured here.

## Wrong-speaker windows by pick margin on the real pair (T-323, Oct 10)
Question (T-317 idea A): are the enrolled tier's wrong-speaker windows confident wrong picks that the margin hold never sees, so that a
sticky pick (`HEARING_PICK_STICKY`) would help? **Answer: no. 32 of 42 wrong windows (76 %) have a pick margin below the hold threshold, and
all 10 above it were gated anyway. Most wrong-speaker time is mix-back from gated windows (34 of 42 windows, 11.4 of 14.4 s): the pick's LDA
score fell below contrast 0, the stream was muted, and the mixer played the whole mix only 6 dB down (`confidence_floor_db(0)`).** Step 2
(the sticky knob) was skipped, as the task required. `hearing/tse/enrolled.py` and `config/demo-team.yaml` are unchanged.

**How it ran.** The live telemetry has no per-window margin, so `eval/wrong_speaker_audit.py` replays `EnrolledCandidate` offline with the
live defaults (1.0 s window, 0.5 s hop, saved profiles aryan/joseph, closed-set LDA, `margin` 0.1, giving a hold threshold of 0.4 LDA units, contrast 0, F0 veto on).
It runs on the T-284 scenes' `stems/mix.wav` and adds the live `tse>mixer` step at focus_hq's slider 100: stream + (window mix - stream) at
the confidence floor. Every window logs `scores`, `margin_score`, `held`, `vetoed`, `gated`, `confidence`, the stream the previous choice
continues (`follow`, what a sticky pick would keep) and the oracle stream (the one closer to the clean target stem than to the other's; for
evaluation only). A window is **wrong** when one of its streamed output's 200 ms frames is other-dominant by the `session_score` rule.
Accuracy-only: no wall-clock numbers, so it ran without `swarm-lock`. Host: laptop-a (`Aryans-MacBook-Pro.local`, M4 Pro, MPS), git `7b82a60`.

```
F=$HEARING_FIXTURES_DIR
.venv/bin/python -m eval.wrong_speaker_audit --profiles $F/profiles \
  --scene joseph:aryan:$F/t141/joseph/demo-team-easy --scene aryan:joseph:$F/t141/aryan/demo-team-easy \
  --scene joseph:aryan:$F/t141/joseph/demo-team-hard --scene aryan:joseph:$F/t141/aryan/demo-team-hard
```

**The replay matches live.** Other-dominant time offline is 4.6 / 1.8 / 5.6 / 2.4 s. Live (T-284 `session_score`, which includes the 3.9 s
cold start) was 4.2 / 2.6 / 5.6 / 2.6 s. The separated stream alone (no residual) is other-dominant for only 0.0 / 1.0 / 0.6 / 1.2 s.

Causes, one per wrong window: `mix_back` = the oracle stream was picked and is clean on its own, and the residual played back carries the other
voice. `separator` = the oracle stream was picked but itself carries the other voice (T-317 idea C). `high margin` = a wrong pick with margin
>= hold. `low margin` = a wrong pick with margin < hold. `sticky fixes` = wrong picks where `follow` was the oracle stream.

| Scene | target:other | other-dominant live (T-284) | offline | stream alone | wrong windows | margin >= hold | < hold, hold did not change the pick | < hold, hold fired (`held`) | gated | mix_back | separator | high margin | low margin | sticky fixes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| demo-team-easy | joseph:aryan | 4.2 s | 4.6 s | 0.0 s | 15 | 5 | 10 | 0 | 15 | 11 | 0 | 1 | 3 | 2/4 |
| demo-team-easy | aryan:joseph | 2.6 s | 1.8 s | 1.0 s | 5 | 0 | 3 | 2 | 1 | 2 | 0 | 0 | 3 | 0/3 |
| demo-team-hard | joseph:aryan | 5.6 s | 5.6 s | 0.6 s | 16 | 5 | 11 | 0 | 15 | 11 | 1 | 1 | 3 | 2/4 |
| demo-team-hard | aryan:joseph | 2.6 s | 2.4 s | 1.2 s | 6 | 0 | 4 | 2 | 3 | 2 | 2 | 0 | 2 | 0/2 |
| **total** | | 15.0 s | 14.4 s | 2.8 s | **42** | **10 (24 %)** | **28** | **4** | 34 | 26 | 3 | 2 | 11 | 4/13 |

By margin bucket: **margin >= hold (10 windows)**: all 10 gated, i.e. even the best stream sat on the other profile's side. 7 are mix_back,
2 high-margin wrong picks, 1 separator. **Margin < hold, hold did not change the pick (28)**: 24 gated. 18 are mix_back, 8 low-margin wrong
picks, 2 separator. **Margin < hold, hold fired (4)**: 3 cases where the hold followed the previous stream onto the wrong voice, plus 1 mix_back.

What it says. (1) The sticky pick has almost nothing to act on. Only 13 of 42 wrong windows are wrong picks, 2 of them high-margin. Keeping
the previous stream would have been right in 4 of the 13. In 3 of the 4 wrong windows where the hold fired, following the previous
stream is what made the pick wrong. (2) The separator is not the main fault here either (3 of 42 windows; the stream alone is
other-dominant for 2.8 s of 14.4 s). (3) The fault is the gated window on the **joseph-as-target** rows: 30 of 31 joseph-row wrong windows
are gated. In 22 of those 30 the target was talking in the window (the LDA put joseph's stream on aryan's side). In the other 8, joseph was
silent and the gate was right, but the 6 dB residual floor let aryan through.

Pointer, not a measurement against the T-180 bar (offline, same command): `HEARING_GATED_FLOOR_DB=20` (T-180's K2) gives other-dominant
0.0 / 1.6 / 0.6 / 1.2 s (14.4 to 3.4 s in total), with offline true dropouts unchanged (0.00 / 0.60 / 0.00 / 0.38 s).
`HEARING_GATED_PLAY=stream` (K5) gives 4.2 / 1.8 / 5.8 / 2.4 s, i.e. no help. T-180 measured K2 live only with aryan as target (leak +0.4 / +2.0 dB,
+0.5 s true dropouts, failing the bar), and this offline replay does not reproduce that dropout cost (no AGC, no overlap-add, no cold start).
So the next measurement is a live T-284-command A/B of K2 on the four rows, joseph-as-target included, against the T-180 bar.
(Done live by T-326, next section: floor 20 passes the T-180 bar, joseph rows 14.8 -> 1.4 % and 19.7 -> 2.8 % wrong-speaker.)
Raw per-window JSON: `$HEARING_RESULTS_DIR/wrong_speaker_audit_20261010-015949.json` (default),
`wrong_speaker_audit_20261010-020143.json` (K2), `wrong_speaker_audit_20261010-015714.json` (K5, before the dropout column was added).

## Gated-window floor live A/B on the real pair (`HEARING_GATED_FLOOR_DB`, Oct 10)
T-326. Question: does T-180's knob K2 (`HEARING_GATED_FLOOR_DB`: the residual floor of a window the extractor gated, confidence exactly 0)
cut wrong-speaker time live on the T-284 stage config? Without the knob that floor is `confidence_floor_db(0)` = 6 dB. **Answer: yes, with
joseph as target, and nothing changes with aryan as target. Floor 20: wrong-speaker 14.8 -> 1.4 % (easy) and 19.7 -> 2.8 % (hard), leak 9.5 / 5.8 dB better,
true dropouts +0.00 s, 0 deadline misses. By the T-180 bar 12 and 20 both pass; 20 is the best setting.** The cost shows only in a check outside the bar
(below): on the joseph rows, target-active time pushed down by >= 10 dB rises by 2.8-4.2 s. Listen before adopting it. `config/demo-team.yaml` is unchanged.

**How it ran.** One batch, laptop-a (`Aryans-MacBook-Pro.local`, Apple M4 Pro, MPS, local backend, no `HEARING_REMOTE_URL`), git `d2b11545`,
2026-10-10 02:17:34-02:25:29 local: `swarm-lock --exclusive --max-load 4 --wait 1500 -- bash .swarm-run/batch.sh`. The lock waited 206 s for two verify runs.
The 12 rows ran one after another, each with its own server. Every row is the exact T-284 command
(`HEARING_PROFILES_DIR=$HEARING_FIXTURES_DIR/profiles HEARING_REGRESS_NO_BASELINE=1 .venv/bin/python scripts/demo_regress.py --modes focus_hq
--seconds 30 --no-free --no-baseline --profile team --isolation standard --voice-profile <target>:<other> --clip $HEARING_FIXTURES_DIR/t141/<target>/demo-team-<scene>`).
The only change is `HEARING_GATED_FLOOR_DB=12` or `=20` in front, or no variable (`unset`). Columns as in T-284. `wrong-speaker` = `other_dominant_s` / 28.4 s.
`enrolled share` = the share of all windows (the share after the 3.8-3.9 s cold start, in brackets). `deadline misses` = enrolled-health `overruns + late` / `windows`.
SI-SDRi is on `out.wav`, with pre-AGC in brackets. Δ is against the `unset` row of the same scene in this batch. Batch log and per-row extraction:
`$HEARING_RESULTS_DIR/T-326_gated_floor_20261010-0217.{log,jsonl}`; session JSON `$HEARING_RESULTS_DIR/demo_regress_<run>.json`.

The `unset` rows reproduce T-284's numbers to the last digit (same clips, same profiles, deterministic pipeline).

**Verdict against the T-180 bar.** The bar: wrong-speaker not up on any row; leak >= 3 dB better or wrong-speaker clearly down; true dropouts +<= 0.2 s per row; 0 new deadline misses.
- `HEARING_GATED_FLOOR_DB=12`: **passes.** Wrong-speaker is down on both joseph rows (-6.3 / -10.5 pp) and flat on both aryan rows. Leak is 4.0 / 3.1 dB better on the joseph rows. Dropouts +0.00 s on every row, deadline misses 0 / 58 everywhere.
- `HEARING_GATED_FLOOR_DB=20`: **passes, and is the best setting.** Wrong-speaker on the joseph rows falls to 1.4 % and 2.8 % (-13.4 / -16.9 pp), and leak is 9.5 / 5.8 dB better. The aryan rows are flat (within 0.16 dB). Dropouts +0.00 s, deadline misses 0 / 58. SI-SDRi after the AGC goes up on the joseph rows (+0.90 / +1.10 dB).
- **Best setting: 20**, with the listening caveat below. T-180 measured K2 live with aryan as target only and saw +0.5 s dropouts. Here the aryan rows did not move at all (dropouts 0.06 / 0.10 s in all three settings).

**Why the aryan rows do not move.** Gated windows are rare there. Counting enrolled-tier telemetry ticks with `tse.confidence == 0`: easy-aryan 0-1 of
~253, hard-aryan 0-1 of ~254. On the joseph rows it is 53-55 of ~255 (easy) and 73-74 of ~255 (hard). So K2 has nothing to act on with aryan as target.
The remaining 2.6 s of aryan wrong-speaker time comes from windows with confidence > 0, which this knob does not touch. Rescoring every session with `skip_s=3.9` leaves
`other_dominant_s` unchanged in all 12 rows (24.4 s scored), so none of the wrong-speaker time falls in the cold start. Per-row data: `$HEARING_RESULTS_DIR/T-326_gated_floor_after_cold_20261010-0217.json`.

**The cost the bar does not see (a check outside the bar, informational).** The true-dropout metric counts only 20 ms frames where the output is > 25 dB under the
target. A gated window that holds target speech plays the target 6 dB down unset, 12 dB down at floor 12 and 20 dB down at floor 20. That is a real
attenuation, but the true-dropout metric does not count it. A per-200 ms projection of the pre-AGC output onto the placed target stem (frames with clear target energy, after the
cold start; ~17.8 s per row) measured target-active time with the target >= 10 dB below its input level:

| Scene | target | unset | floor 12 | floor 20 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| easy | joseph | 0.8 s | 3.6 s | 3.8 s |
| easy | aryan | 2.2 s | 2.2 s | 2.2 s |
| hard | joseph | 2.0 s | 5.8 s | 6.2 s |
| hard | aryan | 6.8 s | 6.8 s | 6.8 s |

The median target gain is unchanged in every row (-1.11 / -0.69 / -4.64 / -3.80 dB). So on the joseph rows, K2 trades about 13-17 pp of wrong-speaker time for about 3-4 s of the
target played 10-20 dB down. Most of that cost comes at 12 already (12 -> 20 adds only 0.2-0.4 s). This check is new and was not part of T-180's bar. Data:
`$HEARING_RESULTS_DIR/T-326_target_atten_20261010-0217.json`. Not done: a listening pass on these 12 `out.wav` files (they are under
`results/sessions/regress-20261010-02*`). That pass decides whether the quieter target in gated windows sounds like a dropout. Verdict record: `docs/decisions/T-GATED-FLOOR.md`.

## Negative gate contrast (`HEARING_ENROLLED_CONTRAST`) with and without the gated floor 20, real pair (T-338, Oct 10)
T-338. Question: T-323's audit found that on the joseph-target rows both separated streams score on aryan's side of the LDA in every gated
window (picked stream -0.01 to -0.45, median -0.12), so the gate at contrast 0 mutes joseph in windows where the picked stream is the oracle.
Does a negative contrast (`HEARING_ENROLLED_CONTRAST`, read by `EnrolledCandidate` when the pipeline spec sets none) fix wrong-speaker time
without the target attenuation that floor 20 alone costs (T-326: +3.0 / +4.2 s of target >= 10 dB down on the joseph rows)? Zero-code: two
existing env knobs, no default or config changed (`config/demo-team.yaml` untouched).

**Step 1, offline** (`eval.wrong_speaker_audit`, the T-323 replay: real weights, saved profiles, 1.0 s windows every 0.5 s, the live
`tse>mixer` at slider 100; no AGC, no cold start), laptop-a, MPS, 6 runs in parallel (accuracy only, no lock), 2026-10-10 12:37-12:48:
`HEARING_ENROLLED_CONTRAST=<c> [HEARING_GATED_FLOOR_DB=20] .venv/bin/python -m eval.wrong_speaker_audit --profiles $HEARING_FIXTURES_DIR/profiles
--scene joseph:aryan:$F/t141/joseph/demo-team-easy --scene aryan:joseph:$F/t141/aryan/demo-team-easy --scene joseph:aryan:$F/t141/joseph/demo-team-hard
--scene aryan:joseph:$F/t141/aryan/demo-team-hard --out $HEARING_RESULTS_DIR/T-338_audit_c<c>_f<none|20>.json`.
The `unset` and `floor 20` control rows are T-323's runs `wrong_speaker_audit_20261010-015949.json` / `-020143.json` (same command).
Cells: other-dominant seconds (out of ~30 s) / true dropouts s / gated windows with the target talking (of 59). Per-window rows in the JSON.

| contrast | floor | easy joseph | easy aryan | hard joseph | hard aryan | total other-dominant | total dropouts |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 0 (unset) | unset | 4.6 / 0.00 / 17 | 1.8 / 0.60 / 1 | 5.6 / 0.00 / 22 | 2.4 / 0.38 / 1 | 14.4 s | 0.98 s |
| 0 (unset) | 20 | 0.0 / 0.00 / 17 | 1.6 / 0.60 / 1 | 0.6 / 0.00 / 22 | 1.2 / 0.38 / 1 | 3.4 s | 0.98 s |
| -0.15 | unset | 2.4 / 0.10 / 6 | 1.6 / 0.60 / 0 | 3.0 / 0.08 / 6 | 1.2 / 0.38 / 0 | 8.2 s | 1.16 s |
| -0.15 | 20 | 0.4 / 0.10 / 6 | 1.6 / 0.60 / 0 | 1.4 / 0.08 / 6 | 1.2 / 0.38 / 0 | 4.6 s | 1.16 s |
| -0.3 | unset | 1.4 / 0.10 / 1 | 2.0 / 0.34 / 0 | 1.2 / 0.08 / 2 | 2.0 / 0.26 / 0 | 6.6 s | 0.78 s |
| -0.3 | 20 | 0.6 / 0.10 / 1 | 2.0 / 0.34 / 0 | 1.0 / 0.08 / 2 | 2.0 / 0.26 / 0 | 5.6 s | 0.78 s |
| -0.45 | unset | 1.0 / 0.10 / 1 | 2.0 / 0.34 / 0 | 2.2 / 0.08 / 0 | 2.0 / 0.26 / 0 | 7.2 s | 0.78 s |
| -0.45 | 20 | 0.8 / 0.10 / 1 | 2.0 / 0.34 / 0 | 2.2 / 0.08 / 0 | 2.0 / 0.26 / 0 | 7.0 s | 0.78 s |

What it says offline. A negative contrast removes the gated-while-talking windows that floor 20 turns into 20 dB attenuation (17 / 22 at
contrast 0 -> 6 / 6 at -0.15 -> 1 / 2 at -0.3), which is the cost T-326 found live. It also changes picks on the aryan rows: at -0.3 and
below, easy-aryan other-dominant goes 1.8 -> 2.0 s (+0.2 s, against "wrong-speaker not up on any row") while its dropouts fall 0.60 -> 0.34 s.
-0.45 adds wrong picks on hard-joseph (2.2 s) with no gain. Floor 20 on top of a negative contrast still helps joseph rows where windows stay gated.
**Live picks (the best 2):** `-0.15` with no floor (the only setting that is down or flat on every row vs unset and keeps few gated windows),
and `-0.3` + floor 20 (lowest joseph-row wrong-speaker with only 1-2 gated-while-talking windows, i.e. little room for the floor's attenuation).
`-0.15` + floor 20 has the lower offline total (4.6 s) but keeps 6 / 6 gated-while-talking windows at 20 dB, about a third of floor 20's
measured +3.0 / +4.2 s attenuation cost, so it was not taken live.

**Step 2, live** (the exact T-284 command, the knob in front; one `swarm-lock --exclusive --max-load 4 --wait 1200 -- bash .swarm-run/batch.sh`
call, laptop-a, Apple M4 Pro, MPS, local backend, git `a05b9c6b` = main `2fdc1f28` + this task's docs, 2026-10-10 13:08:57-13:14:20 local).
**Caveat:** the lock waited the full 1200 s behind the T-339, T-336 and T-337 measurements and then ran **without** the exclusive slot
(`no verify slot after 1200 s; running without one`), at 1-min load 3.85-8.61. The quality columns are deterministic (T-326's `unset` rows
reproduce T-284 to the digit). The wall-clock columns are not: p95 compute 237-349 ms (vs ~255 ms in T-326), and the 4 + 1 deadline misses on the
`-0.15` hard rows come with that load, not from the knob (the knob changes no compute). Controls = T-326's `unset` and `floor 20` rows (same command, git `d2b11545`).
Telemetry confirmed the knob reached the server: every enrolled tick reported `contrast` -0.15 / -0.3. Columns as in T-326; `target >=10 dB down`
= target-active time (200 ms frames, target stem > -38 dBFS, after the 3.9 s cold start, ~17.8-18.0 s per row) where the pre-AGC output's
least-squares target gain is >= 10 dB below the input's. That re-implementation (`.swarm-run/atten.py`, scratch) reproduces T-326's 0.8 / 2.2 / 2.0 / 6.8 s
(unset) and 3.8 / 6.2 s (floor 20, joseph) exactly. Δ is against the `unset` row. Batch log and per-row extraction:
`$HEARING_RESULTS_DIR/T-338_neg_contrast_live.{log,jsonl}`; session JSON `$HEARING_RESULTS_DIR/demo_regress_<run>.json`.

**Verdict.** Bar = T-180 (wrong-speaker not up on any row vs unset; leak >= 3 dB better or wrong-speaker clearly down; true dropouts +<= 0.2 s;
0 new deadline misses) **and** target >= 10 dB down within +1.0 s of unset on every row.
- `contrast -0.15` (floor unset): **fails.** It is the only setting here with wrong-speaker clearly down on all four rows (-7.8 / -2.2 / -7.0 / -4.3 pp)
  and dropouts are within +0.16 s, but target >= 10 dB down rises +4.0 s (easy joseph) and +1.6 s (hard joseph). Its 4 + 1 deadline misses came under load 4.4-5.6 without the slot; not attributed to the knob.
- `contrast -0.3 + floor 20`: **passes T-180, fails the attenuation guard** (+4.0 / +4.2 s on the joseph rows, the same as floor 20 alone: 4.8 vs 3.8 s and 6.2 vs 6.2 s).
  Wrong-speaker is down on every row (-12.0 / -0.7 / -16.9 / -0.7 pp), leak is 8.5 / 4.8 dB better on the joseph rows, dropouts +<= 0.18 s, 0 / 58 deadline misses.
  On the aryan rows leak is 0.8 / 1.8 dB worse while wrong-speaker is only 0.2 s lower.
- **Best setting tried: `HEARING_ENROLLED_CONTRAST=-0.3` + `HEARING_GATED_FLOOR_DB=20`**, but it is no better than floor 20 alone on the joseph rows
  (wrong-speaker 2.8 / 2.8 % vs 1.4 / 2.8 %, same attenuation) and costs aryan-row leak. **No setting meets the attenuation guard, so nothing here
  should be adopted; `config/demo-team.yaml` and every default are unchanged.**

Why the offline proxy missed it. Offline, a negative contrast cut the gated-while-talking windows 17 / 22 -> 6 / 6 (-0.15) and 1 / 2 (-0.3), and
those windows are where floor 20 attenuates joseph. Live, `-0.15` alone still plays joseph >= 10 dB down for 4.8 s on the easy row. The likely reason (not
verified per window here): an ungated window is played at its confidence floor (6-40 dB), so a wrong or low-confidence pick ducks joseph at least as
much as the gated floor did. If so, the attenuation cost comes from the pick, not only from the gated floor, and a contrast shift cannot remove it.
The fix has to change which stream plays (T-336's team-trained classifier) or keep the floor soft when the target is talking (T-337's overlap-aware floor).

Listening files (level-matched to `2-today` on active speech, gain +0.84 / +0.58 dB, within 0.7 / 1.1 dB of `3-floor20`):
`~/Desktop/selective-hearing-floor20/joseph-{easy,hard}-4-contrast-0.3.wav` (contrast -0.3 + floor 20; README.txt there updated). Not done: the human listen.
Verdict record: `docs/decisions/T-NEG-CONTRAST.md`.

## Old vs new voice profiles on held-out new-session scenes (+ floor 20, + retrained team head, + mixed pair; T-368, Oct 10)
T-368. Question: the old aryan/joseph profiles came from a close-mic phone memo (session 1), and T-323 found Joseph's separated crops scoring on
Aryan's side. Do profiles re-enrolled from the Oct 10 demo-mic takes (MacBook Pro Microphone at demo distance) fix that? Tested alone, with
`HEARING_GATED_FLOOR_DB=20` (T-326), with the T-336 team head retrained on separated crops of the new takes, and as a mixed pair (new Joseph +
old Aryan, Q-1160). `config/` is unchanged.

**Verdict: the best setting measured is the mixed pair (`HEARING_PROFILES_DIR=$HEARING_FIXTURES_DIR/profiles-v2/mixed`) +
`HEARING_GATED_FLOOR_DB=20`.** Over all 8 held-out scenes it cuts wrong-speaker time 27.2 -> 13.2 s, against today's old profiles with no knob.
That ties old + floor 20 for the lowest. Its target >= 10 dB down time is 14.2 s against 15.2 s today, where old + floor 20 adds 11.8 s. It has the
best pre-AGC SI-SDRi (+8.94 vs +5.66 dB) and no row adds more than +1.0 s of target attenuation. Joseph as target is fixed: 20.8 -> 4.8 s over his
four scenes. **It still fails the T-342 bar on two counts, so the default should not switch on this run alone.** First, wrong-speaker rises on three of
the four aryan-target rows (+0.2 / +1.2 / +1.2 s). Second, there is one new late window (1 / 58 on aryan `holdout-sir0-cafe`). The orchestrator
decides adoption with Aryan after listening: `~/Desktop/selective-hearing-v2/`.

**Profiles.** `bash scripts/enroll_profiles.sh --out $HEARING_FIXTURES_DIR/profiles-v2 ... aryan profile-02..07 joseph profile-02..07` (the T-172
path; `profiles/` untouched). Version `v1` uses all six 2-min takes (614 s of speech per person). It is **unusable live**: the enrolled tier spends
~19 s `enrolling` at every session start, so it played in only 35 % of windows (`demo_regress_20261010-163847`, NOT MEASURED, run stopped). Version
`v2` (current, measured below) is a 10 s cut (20-30 s) of each of the six takes through the same command with `--force` (a 10 s take fails the 20 s
length check). That is about 45 s of speech per person, close to the old profiles' 50 s. ECAPA cosine (enroll_profiles' same-voice check, must be < 0.7):

| profiles | source | cos(aryan, joseph) |
|---|---|---|
| `profiles/` (old) | phone voice memo, session 1, `profile-01.wav` | 0.019 |
| `profiles-v2/v1` | Oct 10 demo mic, profile-02..07 whole | 0.411 |
| `profiles-v2/v2` (measured) | Oct 10 demo mic, 10 s of each of profile-02..07 | 0.396 |

The new pair is MORE alike than the old one (0.40 vs 0.02). The old profiles differed mostly by channel (two phones, close mic), which is why
Joseph's demo-mic crops did not match his own old profile. Both stay far below the 0.7 same-voice limit.

**Mixed pair (Q-1160).** `$HEARING_FIXTURES_DIR/profiles-v2/mixed/` holds two symlinks: `aryan -> profiles/aryan` (old phone-memo profile) and
`joseph -> profiles-v2/joseph` (new demo-mic profile, `v2`). Each role gets the profile that served it better in attempt 1. Rows `mixed` and
`mixed+floor20` set `HEARING_PROFILES_DIR` to it. `list_profiles(profiles-v2)` does not list `mixed` (no wav and no `current` file at its top level).

**Head retrain (v3).** `python -m eval.oct10_reenroll train-head` uses T-336's recipe: `eval/train_team_head.window_rows`, aryan:joseph ratios
+10..-10 dB plus single-talker takes, babble at SNR 5 dB, and label = the target stem dominates the separated stream by >= 3 dB. It ran on one 15 s
segment (20-35 s) of **each of the six takes** profile-02..07: 42 training scenes, 4872 streams, 1001 positive. LDA features come from
`profiles-v2` (`current` = `v2`), the profiles it is applied with live. Train accuracy 0.872 (LDA 0.625), false accept 0.041 (LDA 0.422), 387 s.
Weights: `$HEARING_RESULTS_DIR/T-368_team_head_v3.json`. Attempt 1's `T-368_team_head_v2.json` (3 takes, LDA from `v1`) is superseded, and none of
its rows remain in the table.

**Scenes** (`$HEARING_FIXTURES_DIR/t368/<target>/holdout-sir{0,-6}-{none,cafe}`, `scenes_manifest.json`; `python -m eval.oct10_reenroll scenes`).
Built ONLY from `team/<name>/holdout-01/02.wav`, which were never used for enrollment or training, with `eval.demo_scene.compose` in the T-141 shape
(target alone, turns, overlap, target alone; ~30 s, target face left on a grey card). SIR 0 or -6 dB (at -6 dB the other talker is 6 dB LOUDER,
on the other stem throughout, as in T-340's tuning scenes). Either no noise or DEMAND PCAFETER cafeteria noise at SNR 10 dB under the target.
Both target roles: 8 scenes.

**How it ran.** One `swarm-lock --exclusive --max-load 4 --wait 1500 -- bash .swarm-run/batch2.sh` on laptop-a (`Aryans-MacBook-Pro.local`,
Apple M4 Pro, MPS, local backend, no `HEARING_REMOTE_URL`). The lock was held 19:11:04-19:48:44 local, after waiting 16.5 min behind T-382's
verify_full and T-386's measurement. It ran **56 rows, every one exit 0**: all 7 conditions on all 8 scenes (`batch --labels mixed,mixed+floor20,new+head+floor20 --sirs=0`, then `batch --sirs=-6`, then `batch --labels old,old+floor20,new,new+floor20 --sirs=0`, appended to the running script at 19:27 so that the SIR 0 old/new rows are re-measured in the same session). Each row is the exact T-284 command
on a held-out scene. Attempt 1's batch (16:50-17:09) ran at 1-min load 2.8-11.4 and p95 compute 410-1200 ms, and was stopped after 26 rows. All
of its rows are re-measured here and superseded. For example, old aryan `holdout-sir-6-cafe` read 15.0 % wrong-speaker there and 4.9 % here.
The 1-min load at row start was 2.7-7.4 (logged per row), and p95 compute was 239-389 ms per window, except for the one loaded row noted below.

```
HEARING_PROFILES_DIR=$HEARING_FIXTURES_DIR/<profiles|profiles-v2|profiles-v2/mixed> [HEARING_GATED_FLOOR_DB=20] \
  [HEARING_TEAM_HEAD=$HEARING_RESULTS_DIR/T-368_team_head_v3.json] \

  --voice-profile <target>:<other> --clip $HEARING_FIXTURES_DIR/t368/<target>/<scene>
```

**Totals over all 8 scenes** (seconds summed, leak and pre-AGC SI-SDRi averaged; deadline misses out of 464 windows):

| profiles + knob | wrong-speaker | target >= 10 dB down | true dropouts | leak | SI-SDRi pre-AGC | deadline misses | rows over the T-342 bar (wrong-speaker up / atten > +1.0 s) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| old (today) | 27.2 s | 15.2 s | 0.94 s | -10.91 dB | +5.66 dB | 2 | reference |
| old + floor 20 | **13.2 s** | 27.0 s | 0.94 s | **-13.41 dB** | +6.46 dB | **0** | 0 / 4 (joseph +1.4 to +5.2 s) |
| new | 21.4 s | **12.8 s** | 1.50 s | -11.30 dB | +7.89 dB | 2 | 4 (aryan) / 1 |
| new + floor 20 | 16.8 s | 14.8 s | 1.54 s | -12.04 dB | +7.88 dB | 39 (38 on the loaded row) | 4 (aryan) / 1 |
| new + head v3 + floor 20 | 18.6 s | 17.6 s | **0.54 s** | -11.97 dB | +8.39 dB | 1 | 3 (aryan) / 2 |
| mixed | 17.8 s | **11.2 s** | 0.76 s | -12.14 dB | +8.52 dB | 8 | 4 (aryan) / 0 |
| **mixed + floor 20** | **13.2 s** | 14.2 s | 0.80 s | -13.27 dB | **+8.94 dB** | 1 | 3 (aryan, +0.2 to +1.2 s) / 0 |

Per target role (wrong-speaker / target >= 10 dB down, 4 scenes each):

| profiles + knob | aryan target | joseph target |
|---|---|---|
| old (today) | **6.4 / 12.0 s** | 20.8 / 3.2 s |
| old + floor 20 | 6.4 / 12.0 s | 6.8 / 15.0 s |
| new | 12.6 / 6.8 s | 8.8 / 6.0 s |
| new + floor 20 | 10.4 / 8.0 s | 6.4 / 6.8 s |
| new + head v3 + floor 20 | 10.4 / 10.4 s | 8.2 / 7.2 s |
| mixed | 12.0 / 6.6 s | 5.8 / 4.6 s |
| mixed + floor 20 | 8.4 / 9.6 s | **4.8 / 4.6 s** |

**What the numbers say.**
- **Joseph as target (the T-323 fault) is fixed by his new profile.** Wrong-speaker falls 20.8 -> 8.8 s (new) and 5.8 s (mixed) with no added
  target attenuation; mixed + floor 20 gives 4.8 s. Old + floor 20 also reaches 6.8 s, but only by holding the target >= 10 dB down 11.8 s longer
  (T-326's finding again).
- **Aryan as target is best with today's old pair** (6.4 s). Every setting that uses Joseph's new profile makes the aryan rows worse: the
  aryan-target decision uses Joseph's profile as its negative, and Joseph's new profile sits closer to Aryan's voice. That holds even when Aryan
  keeps his old profile (mixed: 12.0 s). Floor 20 recovers part of it (mixed + floor 20: 8.4 s); the floor changes nothing on the old pair's aryan
  rows, which have no gated windows.
- **The retrained head (v3) does not help live.** Offline it cuts target-absent accepts to 0-3 s from 3.5-24 s (table). Live, though, it adds
  gated windows (26-49 per row), more target attenuation (17.6 s) and more aryan wrong-speaker than new + floor 20 on the no-noise scenes. As in
  T-342, the head works offline but fails the bar live.
- **Target-absent accepts** show why the LDA decision alone is fragile in both directions: with the old pair, Joseph-alone audio is accepted as
  Aryan in 29.0-29.5 s of 29.5 s, and with the new pair Aryan-alone audio is accepted as Joseph in 23.0-24.0 s. The mixed pair sits between
  them (aryan target 15.0-19.0 s, joseph target 8.0-23.5 s); head v3 accepts 0.0-3.0 s. The offline number does not track the live one: the
  old pair's aryan rows have the most target-absent accepts and the least live wrong-speaker. Live, the enrolled stream
  is still chosen correctly most of the time because the extraction is conditioned on the target, but this is the margin the head (or a
  per-role pair) would have to fix.

**Next.** The data point to a per-role pair: today's old pair when Aryan is the target, and Joseph's new profile when Joseph is. `profiles/`
is shared by both roles, so that needs a code change (a profile set per target), which is outside this task. Until then, mixed + floor 20 is the
best single setting. Listening files: `~/Desktop/selective-hearing-v2/` (`<target>-{0-input-mix,1-old-profiles-today,2-new-profiles-today,
3-best-mixed-floor20,4-new-floor20}.wav`, level-matched, README). Decision record: `docs/decisions/T-368.md`.
## Broad knob sweep: tuning set apart from the test scenes, confirmed on the real pair (T-340, Oct 10)
Question: tuned together, do the existing knobs (`HEARING_ENROLLED_CONTRAST`, `HEARING_GATED_FLOOR_DB`, `HEARING_UNSURE_DB`,
`HEARING_GATED_PLAY`) beat the stage defaults (contrast 0, all floors unset, gated windows silent)? The bar is T-180's plus two checks: target attenuation within +1.0 s of unset on every row, and 0 new deadline misses.
**Verdict: keep the current defaults.** The tuning set's two best settings (contrast -0.15, with or without `HEARING_GATED_PLAY=stream`) cut live wrong-speaker time on all 8 rows (-2.2 to -9.8 pp), but both fail the bar on two checks. Target attenuation rises +4.2 / +2.0 s (contrast -0.15) and +3.8 / +2.0 s (with stream) on the joseph rows, against a +1.0 s limit. And each setting adds one late window (1 / 58). No setting in the 72-point grid kept the attenuation within +1.0 s on the joseph scenes while cutting wrong-speaker time by much.

**Tuning set (does not overlap the test scenes).** `eval/knob_sweep.py build` writes 12 scenes to `$HEARING_FIXTURES_DIR/tuning_t340/`
(`tuning_manifest.json` holds the provenance check). The layout is the same as `demo_scene` (`stems/{mix,target,other,noise}.wav` + `manifest.json`). Each scene is 24 s: target alone 0-4 s,
2 s alternating turns 4-12 s, overlap 12-20 s, target alone 20-24 s. Grid: target aryan or joseph × overlap SIR 0 / -5 / -10 dB (the other talker that much
louder) × no noise or DEMAND `PCAFETER/ch01.wav` cafeteria noise at SNR 10 dB. Speech comes ONLY from `team/<name>/profile-01.wav` and the T-306
augmentations of it. Provenance check against `eval/augmented/T-306/manifest.json`: an item is kept only when `source_file == profile-01.wav` **and**
`source_sha256` equals the sha256 of the team profile cut. Kept: aryan 25 items (5 each of clean, reverb_02, reverb_05, opus16, opus32), joseph 10 (2 each).
Refused: every `fixture-01` item (aryan 63, joseph 55: the T-141 test scenes come from those cuts), every `fixture-02-namecall` item (33), and the profile-01 pitch ±2/±4, reverb_08 and combo items (37;
pitch shifts change the voice the profile scores). Pools after a 20 ms energy VAD: aryan 125 speech pieces, joseph 40 pieces of 0.8-2 s.
Caveat: the enrollment profiles (`$HEARING_FIXTURES_DIR/profiles/<name>/profile-01.wav`) are taken from the same recording as these cuts, so the tuning set scores
the pick on audio close to its own enrollment. That flatters absolute pick accuracy. It tunes the knobs on the right voices without touching the test scenes.

**Offline sweep (accuracy only, no swarm-lock).** `.venv/bin/python -m eval.knob_sweep sweep --tuning $HEARING_FIXTURES_DIR/tuning_t340` covers all 72
settings: contrast {0, -0.15, -0.3, -0.45} × gated floor {unset, 12, 20} × unsure floor {unset, 10, 14} × gated play {zero (default), stream}.
It replays `EnrolledCandidate` the way `eval/wrong_speaker_audit.py` does (real weights, saved profiles, 1.0 s windows every 0.5 s; the output is the stream plus the residual at
`confidence_floor_db`). The separation is cached per window, because no knob changes it. Scores per scene: wrong-speaker s (`other_dominant`, the
session_score rule); leak (the other stem's least-squares energy, out vs in); target >= 10 dB down s (T-326's check: 200 ms frames with clear target
energy whose target projection is >= 10 dB under the input's); true dropouts. Run on laptop-a (MPS), git `44f1fd60`, ~3 min. Totals over 12 scenes × 24 s (288 s).

Against the bar:
- T-180 bar: **passes for both.** Wrong-speaker is down on every row. Leak is 2.9-5.4 dB better on the joseph rows. True dropouts are +0.16 s at most (easy-joseph 0.00 -> 0.16 s).
- Target attenuation within +1.0 s of unset on every row: **fails for both.** joseph easy +4.2 / +3.8 s, joseph hard +2.0 / +2.0 s. The aryan rows are at +1.0 s (easy) and +0.2 s (hard).
- 0 new deadline misses: **fails for both.** One late window each (contrast -0.15: easy-joseph; with stream: easy-aryan), at a load of 4-7.5. T-309 also saw one late window at contrast 0.2.
`c-0.15` and `c-0.15+stream` are the same on the aryan rows and the hard-joseph attenuation. Stream gives back 0.4 s of attenuation on easy-joseph, but it is worse on hard-joseph wrong-speaker (12.0 vs 9.9 %).

The trade has the same shape as T-326's floor 20 (why contrast -0.15 attenuates the target more was not diagnosed here). Contrast -0.15 removes 7.8-9.8 pp of joseph-row wrong-speaker
time at a cost of 2-4 s of target-active time played >= 10 dB down. Floor 20 gave 13-17 pp for 3.2-4.4 s. Listening files: `~/Desktop/selective-hearing-floor20/joseph-{easy,hard}-5-sweep.wav`
(the `c-0.15` rows' `out.wav`, RMS over active frames matched to the `joseph-<scene>-2-today.wav` files: +0.75 / +0.05 dB). Compare them with `-2-today` (defaults) and `-3-floor20`. No default or config changed.
Decision record: `docs/decisions/T-KNOB-SWEEP.md`.
## Enrolled tier warmed at server start (`HEARING_WARM_ENROLLED=1`, T-339, Oct 10)
Question: does warming the enrolled tier at server start cut the 3.9 s cold start (T-284) without changing what plays?
**Answer: yes. First isolated drops from 3.83-3.84 s to 1.84-1.87 s (-2.0 s), enrolled share goes from 87.1 % to 93.5-93.6 %,
deadline misses stay 0 / 58, and the separated output is sample-identical to the knob-off run from 3.95 s on.**

**Cause.** The server's warm path (T-072/T-088) already loads MossFormer2 and ECAPA and runs one enrolled window. The 3.2 s of `loading`
came from the stage, not the models. `FocusHQ` submitted `load()` only when its first window was due (1.0 s), so window 0 was skipped
as `loading`. That reason needs `RECOVER_WINDOWS` = 4 clean windows to clear, so the tier stayed unhealthy until about 3.2 s.
**Knob** (`HEARING_WARM_ENROLLED=1` on the server; unset keeps today's code path, `tests/test_warm_enrolled.py`):
(1) a threaded `tse` stage submits its load with its first chunk, so window 0 is computed and only the cold-window `warming up` rule applies
(1 clean window); (2) `hearing.server.warm.warm_enrolled` embeds every saved profile pair once at start into
`hearing.tse.enrolled.WARM_PROFILES` (keyed by encoder instance and clip). It also runs one 1.0 s window with the first pair, so the
closed-set (LDA) path runs once before any session. Sessions then reuse those embeddings instead of a ~1 s ECAPA job each. The pill states
(T-329) are unchanged: the tier reads `warming up` / `loading` until it is really active.

**How it ran.** laptop-a (`Aryans-MacBook-Pro.local`, Apple M4 Pro, MPS, local backend), git `7100614` (this branch on main `2fdc1f2`), 2026-10-10 13:10-13:13,
one call `swarm-lock --exclusive --max-load 4 --wait 1200 -- bash .swarm-run/batch.sh`. It waited ~11 min for T-337's measurement, then ran
two server-start probes (knob off, on) and the four rows one after another. Each row is the exact T-284 command, with
`HEARING_WARM_ENROLLED=1` exported in the `on` rows:
`HEARING_PROFILES_DIR=$HEARING_FIXTURES_DIR/profiles HEARING_REGRESS_NO_BASELINE=1 .venv/bin/python scripts/demo_regress.py --modes focus_hq
--seconds 30 --no-free --no-baseline --profile team --isolation standard --voice-profile <target>:<other> --clip $HEARING_FIXTURES_DIR/t141/<target>/demo-team-hard`.
Columns as in the T-284 table. Per-row extraction and batch log: `$HEARING_RESULTS_DIR/T-339_warm_enrolled_20261010-1310.{jsonl,log}`.

| Scene | target:other | knob | load (1/5/15) | exit | enrolled share | deadline misses | p95 compute (p50) | SI-SDRi (pre-AGC) | leak | wrong-speaker | true dropouts | DNSMOS OVRL | first isolated | Run |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| demo-team-hard | joseph:aryan | off | 5.52 / 5.11 / 5.98 | 0 | 87.1 % | 0 / 58 | 254.8 (239.3) ms | +0.43 (+2.95) dB | -6.49 dB | 19.7 % (5.6 s) | 0.00 s | 2.14 | 3.83 s | `demo_regress_20261010-131053` |
| demo-team-hard | joseph:aryan | **on** | 8.66 / 5.99 / 6.27 | 0 | 93.6 % | 0 / 58 | 346.9 (337.8) ms | +0.83 (+3.32) dB | -6.51 dB | 19.7 % (5.6 s) | 0.00 s | 2.08 | **1.84 s** | `demo_regress_20261010-131134` |
| demo-team-hard | aryan:joseph | off | 7.09 / 5.90 / 6.22 | 0 | 87.1 % | 0 / 58 | 331.9 (327.7) ms | +0.18 (+1.04) dB | -9.66 dB | 9.2 % (2.6 s) | 0.10 s | 2.61 | 3.84 s | `demo_regress_20261010-131215` |
| demo-team-hard | aryan:joseph | **on** | 5.64 / 5.68 / 6.12 | 0 | 93.5 % | 0 / 58 | 336.1 (305.5) ms | +0.39 (+1.22) dB | -9.66 dB | 9.2 % (2.6 s) | 0.10 s | 2.63 | **1.87 s** | `demo_regress_20261010-131256` |

Every row: profile enrollment, `negative_active` true, pair cosine 0.018, contrast 0.0, hop 0.5 s (never widened), delay 1500 ms,
1 tier switch. In the `off` rows the enrolled health reads `loading` until 3.21-3.22 s; in the `on` rows it never reads `loading`.

**Quality is the same output, started earlier.** The knob-off rows match T-284 / T-327 to the digit on every quality column.
In the knob-on rows, wrong-speaker time, true dropouts and leak (within 0.02 dB) are unchanged. The whole-clip SI-SDRi and DNSMOS move
(+0.2 to +0.4 dB SI-SDRi, DNSMOS -0.06 / +0.02) only because 2 s more of the clip is now scored on enrolled output instead of
audio-only. Sample check (`.swarm-run/same.py`, off vs on per target): `out_pre_agc.wav` (the separated stream) is
**bit-identical from 3.95 s to the end** in both scenes. `out.wav` converges once the output AGC's envelope does (identical from
9.11 s for aryan, 16.92 s for joseph; before that it differs by at most 0.05 / 0.15). `in.wav` is identical.

**Server start.** `.swarm-run/startup.py` timed `hearing serve` from launch to `/health` `warm: ready` inside the same lock, with the
profiles dir set. The profile step (`enrolled_profiles`) costs **569-689 ms** (2 profiles). Off/on to ready: 6.52 / 5.79 s here,
4.25 / 4.80 s in the contended batch below; the step's cost is smaller than that run-to-run spread. RSS after warm (one `ps` sample
per server): off 689 MB, on 1817 MB here; off 1643 MB, on 1909 MB in the contended batch. MPS allocations make a single RSS sample
unreliable. The knob itself keeps two unit embeddings and two profile piece arrays (192 floats each), a few KB.

**Wall-clock caveat.** The load was 5.5-8.7, above T-284's ~3.9: other agents' unlocked accuracy jobs ran beside the lock. Compute
p50 moved 239-338 ms between rows with the same work, so the p95 column is load, not the knob. Two earlier batches were discarded.
Batch 1 (12:45-12:49) ran beside T-338's six detached `eval.wrong_speaker_audit` workers: compute p50 1.3 s per window, and three rows
never reached the enrolled tier. Batch 2 (12:54-12:58, `$HEARING_RESULTS_DIR/T-339_warm_enrolled_contended_20261010-1254.log`): load
spiked to 47, one row never reached the enrolled tier, and the `on` rows overran. Its `on` rows still read first isolated 1.98 s / 1.86 s.

**Decision pending.** `config/` and `scripts/demo.sh` are unchanged. With the gate met (first isolated -2.0 s, separated output
identical, 0 new misses), `demo.sh --team` may export `HEARING_WARM_ENROLLED=1` once the orchestrator approves.
## Team head together with gated floor 20 on the real pair (`HEARING_TEAM_HEAD` + `HEARING_GATED_FLOOR_DB=20`, T-342, Oct 10)
T-342. Question: T-336's team head failed live only because its extra rejects play the whole mix at the 6 dB gated floor. Does it pass
with `HEARING_GATED_FLOOR_DB=20` (T-326), where a reject ducks the residual 20 dB instead? **Answer: it fixes wrong-speaker on all four rows
(14.8 / 9.2 / 19.7 / 9.2 % -> 1.4 / 2.1 / 3.5 / 2.8 %), including the aryan rows that floor 20 alone cannot touch, but it FAILS the bar:
on the joseph rows the target is >= 10 dB down 2.8-3.0 s longer than unset (limit +1.0 s), as with floor 20 alone, and every row had one late
window during the cold start (1 / 58, see below). Not adopted; `config/` is unchanged and both knobs stay unset.**

**How it ran.** One batch, laptop-a (`Aryans-MacBook-Pro.local`, Apple M4 Pro, MPS, local backend, no `HEARING_REMOTE_URL`), 2026-10-10
13:39:28-13:43:38 local: `swarm-lock --exclusive --max-load 4 --wait 1500 -- bash .swarm-run/batch.sh` (waited 119 s). T-336 is not on main, so the
batch ran from a scratch worktree of this branch's HEAD (`e9e5fbd6`) with T-336's `hearing/` diff (`team_head.py`, `enrolled.py`) applied
uncommitted; nothing from T-336 is committed here. Every row is the exact T-284 command with `HEARING_GATED_FLOOR_DB=20
HEARING_TEAM_HEAD=$HEARING_RESULTS_DIR/T-336_team_head.json` in front. The same batch re-ran floor 20 alone on both joseph rows as a reproduction check:
wrong-speaker, leak, SI-SDRi and dropouts match T-326 to the digit. Columns as in T-284 / T-326 / T-336. `gated` = enrolled-tier telemetry ticks with
`tse.confidence == 0`. Δ wrong-speaker and Δ dropouts are against the `unset` row of the same scene. Batch log and per-row extraction (all 18 rows below):
`$HEARING_RESULTS_DIR/T-342_head_floor20_20261010-1339.log`, `T-342_rows_20261010-1339.json`.

`target >= 10 dB down` = T-326's check, re-scored with ONE implementation for every row in this table (rows from all three tasks): 200 ms frames after the
3.9 s cold start whose 20 ms target-VAD frames are mostly active (17.8 s joseph / 17.2 s aryan); pre-AGC output aligned with `out.wav`'s delays and
projected on the placed stems; frame gain = target LS gain out / in. It reproduces T-326's active time (17.8 s) and median gains (-1.11 / -4.64 dB) on the
joseph rows exactly; the 10 dB counts differ by up to 0.4 s (T-326's published unset / floor 20: 0.8 / 2.2 / 2.0 / 6.8 s and 3.8 / 2.2 / 6.2 / 6.8 s).
Compare within this table.

**Target-absent accepts (T-336's method).** The floor knob only sets the residual floor of a window whose confidence is exactly 0
(`hearing/fusion/mixer.py` `confidence_floor_db`); it does not enter the accept/gate decision in `EnrolledCandidate.extract`. So head + floor 20 accepts
exactly what the head accepts, and floor 20 alone exactly what the LDA accepts: target-absent accepted seconds are T-336's offline numbers, LDA (unset,
floor 20) -> head (head, head + floor 20): 27.5 -> 0.5 s on both aryan scenes, 7.5 -> 4.0 s (joseph easy) and 7.5 -> 4.5 s (joseph hard). Not re-measured
(by construction; no new offline run).

**Verdict against the bar: FAIL, do not adopt.**
- Wrong-speaker not up on any row, clearly down on the joseph rows: **passes** (-13.4 / -16.2 pp on joseph, -7.1 / -6.4 pp on aryan). Leak 5.3-8.1 dB better on every row.
- Target >= 10 dB-down time within +1.0 s of unset on every row: **fails on both joseph rows** (+3.0 s easy, +2.8 s hard; with T-326's published unset numbers +2.8 / +3.0 s).
  Passes on the aryan rows (+0.6 s easy, -2.8 s hard). On the joseph rows the head changes little against floor 20 alone (3.6 vs 3.4 s, 5.0 vs 6.2 s).
- Target-absent accepts not up: **passes** (by construction, above).
- 0 new deadline misses: **fails as measured.** Every head + floor 20 row has 1 late window (window 3 of 58, at t = 2.28 s, during the cold start before the
  enrolled tier plays at ~3.9 s); p95 compute is 359-371 ms against 252-266 ms. The floor-20-alone rerun in the same batch had 0 / 58 but also a 361.5 ms p95 on
  the hard row, the 1-min load was 3.3-7.3 at row starts (other agents; `--max-load 4` only gates the start of the batch), and T-336's head-alone rows had
  0 / 58 at load 3.0-3.8. The head is a 5-feature logistic regression on CPU, so load is the likely cause, but this batch cannot separate the two.
- True dropouts +<= 0.2 s: **passes** (0.00 s on every row).

**What it buys and what it does not.** With aryan as target the pairing does what T-336 predicted: rejects now duck instead of playing the mix, so
wrong-speaker falls 9.2 -> 2.1 / 2.8 % (floor 20 alone: 9.2 / 9.2 %), leak improves 7.6 / 6.1 dB, SI-SDRi after the AGC rises +1.9 / +3.1 dB and
target-down time does not rise (2.2 -> 2.8 s, 6.4 -> 3.6 s). With joseph as target it is floor 20 alone within noise (wrong-speaker 1.4 / 3.5 % vs
1.4 / 2.8 %, target-down 3.6 / 5.0 s vs 3.4 / 6.2 s), so it inherits floor 20's cost on the joseph rows, which is what fails the bar. Listening files
(level-matched to `2-today`): `~/Desktop/selective-hearing-floor20/joseph-{easy,hard}-6-head-floor20.wav`. Verdict record: `docs/decisions/T-HEAD-FLOOR20.md`.
## Team-tuned decision head on the real pair (`HEARING_TEAM_HEAD`, T-336, Oct 10)
T-336. Question: does a small classifier trained on SEPARATED 1 s crops of our own voices, used in place of the LDA gate
(`scores[choice] < 0` -> gated, confidence = LDA margin / 0.58), cut wrong-speaker time live? Honest label: **tuned to the two teammates'
voices, trained on enrollment cuts, tested on disjoint takes.** **Answer: no. Knob on, wrong-speaker goes UP on all four rows (+0.4 to +3.2 s)
and leak is 0.2-2.8 dB worse, so it fails the T-180 + T-326 bar. Not adopted; `config/demo-team.yaml` is unchanged and the knob stays off.**

**The head.** `hearing/tse/team_head.py`: an L2 logistic regression (numpy Newton steps, CPU, seconds) on five per-stream features:
the live LDA score, the rival stream's LDA score, cosine to the target profile, cosine to the other profile, stream energy in dB re the
louder stream. Accept threshold p >= 0.5. With the knob on, `EnrolledCandidate.extract` (closed-set mode only) replaces the LDA scores with
the head's log-odds minus logit(0.5), so the pick, the hold rule and the contrast-0 gate run on p; the window's confidence is p of the chosen
stream. Unset/empty = the old code path (`tests/test_team_head.py` proves output, scores, confidence and gating are identical).

**Training (enrollment cuts only).** `.venv/bin/python -m eval.train_team_head`: `team/aryan/profile-01.wav` (50 s) and
`team/joseph/profile-01.wav` (15.7 s), cut into three 15 s segments, random talk turns (1-3 s on, 0.5-2 s off) per voice, mixed at
aryan:joseph +10/+5/0/-5/-10 dB (0/-5/-10 dB SIR with either voice as target) plus one single-voice take per voice per segment, over the
committed babble at 5 dB SNR (no café noise bank on main). 1.0 s windows, 0.5 s hop, through the real MossFormer2 separator (MPS) and ECAPA;
LDA from the saved profiles (`$HEARING_FIXTURES_DIR/profiles`) exactly as live. Labels: a stream is "target" when the target stem's
least-squares part beats the other's by >= 3 dB and is >= -45 dBFS; otherwise 0 (other, mixed or silent: the reject class).
2436 streams, 458 positive, 138 s. Training accuracy 91.9 %, false accepts on non-target streams 2.5 % (the raw LDA sign: 61.4 % / 43.0 %).
Coefficients (standardised): lda -0.13, lda_rival -0.48, cos_target +2.41, cos_other -0.91, rel_db +0.86. Weights
`$HEARING_RESULTS_DIR/T-336_team_head.json` (sha256 `43f4a147a65d…`), manifest `docs/decisions/T-TEAM-HEAD.manifest.json`.

**Offline test on the disjoint T-141 scenes (per window, no hold/F0 logic, accuracy only, not load-dependent).** Accept = the argmax stream
passes the gate; "right" = it is target-dominant by the oracle. Target-absent = the scene's `other` + `noise` stems alone (target silent), seconds accepted.

| Scene | target | windows (target in a stream) | LDA accept right / wrong / gated-with-target | head accept right / wrong / gated-with-target | target-absent accepted: LDA -> head |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| demo-team-easy | aryan | 59 (46) | 44 / 14 / 0 | 41 / 1 / 5 | 27.5 s -> 0.5 s |
| demo-team-hard | aryan | 59 (46) | 38 / 18 / 0 | 38 / 0 / 8 | 27.5 s -> 0.5 s |
| demo-team-easy | joseph | 59 (44) | 35 / 3 / 9 | 34 / 1 / 10 | 7.5 s -> 4.0 s |
| demo-team-hard | joseph | 59 (37) | 26 / 7 / 10 | 29 / 6 / 7 | 7.5 s -> 4.5 s |

Offline data: `$HEARING_RESULTS_DIR/T-336_train_20261010.log` (aryan rows; that run was stopped before writing its JSON because the GPU was
contended and the live batch had to start) and `T-336_offline_test_joseph_20261010-1255.json`. Offline the head looks good: wrong accepts
14 -> 1 and 18 -> 0 with aryan as target, and target-absent false accepts are down on every scene (27.5 -> 0.5 s, 7.5 -> 4.0/4.5 s).
It pays with more gated windows that held the target (0 -> 5/8 on the aryan scenes).

**Live A/B (the bar's measurement).** One batch, laptop-a (`Aryans-MacBook-Pro.local`, Apple M4 Pro, MPS, local backend, no `HEARING_REMOTE_URL`),
git `015d6f23` + this branch, 2026-10-10 12:49:00-12:54:20 local: `swarm-lock --exclusive --max-load 4 --wait 1200 -- bash .swarm-run/batch.sh`
(the lock waited ~4 min behind T-339's measurement). Every row is the exact T-284 command; knob on = `HEARING_TEAM_HEAD=$HEARING_RESULTS_DIR/T-336_team_head.json`
in front. Columns as in T-284 / T-326; `gated` = enrolled-tier telemetry ticks with `tse.confidence == 0`; `target >= 10 dB down` = the T-326
target-attenuation check (target-active 200 ms frames after the 3.9 s cold start, pre-AGC output projected on the placed target stem;
re-implemented in this task: it gives 0.8 / 2.8 / 1.4 / 7.0 s on the knob-off rows vs T-326's 0.8 / 2.2 / 2.0 / 6.8 s, so compare off vs on within this table only). Per-row data: `$HEARING_RESULTS_DIR/T-336_live_rows_20261010-1249.json`.
The knob-off rows reproduce T-284's wrong-speaker, leak and dropouts exactly (SI-SDRi within 0.01 dB).

**Verdict against the bar (T-180 + T-326): FAIL, do not adopt.**
- Wrong-speaker not up on any row: **fails on all four** (14.8 -> 18.3 %, 9.2 -> 20.4 %, 19.7 -> 21.1 %, 9.2 -> 19.0 %); overall 15.0 s -> 22.4 s.
- Leak not worse: **fails** (+0.9, +2.8, +0.2, +1.7 dB).
- True dropouts +<= 0.2 s: passes (0.00 s on every knob-on row). Deadline misses: passes (0 / 58 everywhere; p95 compute 253-266 ms).
- Target 10-20 dB attenuation time not up: passes (0.8 -> 0.8, 2.8 -> 0.2, 1.4 -> 1.6, 7.0 -> 0.6 s; +0.2 s on hard-joseph).
- Target-absent false accepts not up: passes offline (27.5 -> 0.5 s aryan scenes, 7.5 -> 4.0 / 4.5 s joseph scenes).

**Why it fails live while it wins offline.** The head rejects more: gated ticks with aryan as target go from 0 to 63 / 78 of ~256. A gated window
plays the whole mix at the 6 dB residual floor (`confidence_floor_db(0)`), and `session_score` counts that mix as other-dominant time, so every
reject the head adds on a crop where the other voice is present becomes wrong-speaker time. The offline table scores a reject as "not a wrong
accept"; the live metric scores it as wrong-speaker. On the windows it does accept the head is cleaner: with aryan as target SI-SDRi rises
+0.9 / +2.6 dB (pre-AGC +2.4 / +4.0 dB), dropouts fall to 0 and target attenuation time falls 2.6 / 6.4 s. With joseph as target it changes
little (gated 55 -> 69, 74 -> 72). Untested next step: the head together with `HEARING_GATED_FLOOR_DB=20` (T-326), where a gated window plays the
residual 20 dB down instead of the mix; that pairing would turn the head's extra rejects into ducking rather than wrong-speaker time. Not measured here
(one knob per task; no listening pass on these 8 `out.wav` files, `results/sessions/regress-20261010-12{49,5}*`). Verdict record: `docs/decisions/T-TEAM-HEAD.md`.
## Overlap-aware gated floor (`HEARING_OVERLAP_FLOOR=1`, T-337, Oct 10): NO-GO
T-337. Question: T-326's `HEARING_GATED_FLOOR_DB=20` plays the target >= 10 dB down for 3-4 s per joseph row. Does keeping that floor
only on gated windows where **both** separated streams are active (a per-stream energy/flatness VAD, `hearing/fusion/stream_vad.py`),
with solo gated windows back at 6 dB, keep the wrong-speaker win and return the target? **Answer: no.** On the joseph rows the target
>= 10 dB-down time does fall, 4.2 -> 1.6 s (easy) and 6.4 -> 3.4 s (hard). But wrong-speaker time goes back up from 1.4 to 14.8 %
(easy) and from 2.8 to 12.0 % (hard). That is +13.4 / +9.2 pp against an allowance of <= 1 pp, so the task's win bar fails.
Verdict: `docs/decisions/T-337-overlap-floor.md`. `config/demo-team.yaml` is unchanged.

**How it ran.** One batch, laptop-a (`Aryans-MacBook-Pro.local`, Apple M4 Pro, MPS, local backend, no `HEARING_REMOTE_URL`), git `61e51d22`,
2026-10-10 13:02:12-13:10:25 local: `swarm-lock --exclusive --max-load 4 --wait 1500 -- bash .swarm-run/batch.sh`. The lock waited about 16 min
for the T-339 and T-336 measurements. The 12 rows ran one after another, each with its own server, and each is the exact T-284 command
(`HEARING_PROFILES_DIR=$HEARING_FIXTURES_DIR/profiles HEARING_REGRESS_NO_BASELINE=1 .venv/bin/python scripts/demo_regress.py --modes focus_hq
--seconds 30 --no-free --no-baseline --profile team --isolation standard --voice-profile <target>:<other> --clip $HEARING_FIXTURES_DIR/t141/<target>/demo-team-<scene>`).
Rows differ only in the environment: none (`unset`), `HEARING_GATED_FLOOR_DB=20` (`20`), or `HEARING_GATED_FLOOR_DB=20 HEARING_OVERLAP_FLOOR=1` (`20 + overlap`).
The columns are the T-326 columns. `deadline misses` = enrolled-health `overruns + late` / `windows` at the last tick. `wrong-speaker` = `other_dominant_s` / 28.4 s.
`target down` is the T-326 check: target-active 200 ms frames after the 3.9 s cold start where the pre-AGC output's projection on the
placed target stem is >= 10 (6) dB under the input's (`eval.wrong_speaker_audit --session`). It reproduces T-326's easy-joseph rows at
0.8 s (unset) and 4.2 s (floor 20); T-326 reported 0.8 and 3.8 s. Δ vs unset = wrong-speaker / leak / dropouts. Δ vs 20 = wrong-speaker / target >= 10 dB down.
The 5-min load was 5-9 for this batch: the machine had just finished other tasks' measurements. T-326's was 3-5. That explains the
higher p95 (up to 375 ms) and the single late windows, which appear in all three arms. The unset rows reproduce T-284/T-326 on every
quality column. Batch log and per-row data: `$HEARING_RESULTS_DIR/T-337_overlap_floor_live_20261010-1302.{log,json}`.

**Verdict.**
- **Against floor 20 alone (the task's win bar): fails.** The target comes back (-2.6 / -3.0 s of >= 10 dB-down time on the joseph rows).
  But wrong-speaker time gives back 13.4 / 9.2 pp. The bar allows <= 1 pp.
- **Against unset (the T-180 bar):** hard joseph passes: wrong-speaker -7.7 pp, leak 2.4 dB better, dropouts +0.00 s, 0 / 58 misses,
  p95 257 ms. Easy joseph adds nothing: wrong-speaker is unchanged, leak 0.6 dB better, and there is one late window (0 + 1 / 58)
  that floor 20 alone also had at this load. The aryan rows are identical in all three arms (as in T-326, aryan-target windows are
  almost never gated). p95 compute stays under 400 ms in every row. The VAD costs 0.55 ms per 1 s window.

**Why: offline audit** (`eval/wrong_speaker_audit.py`, 1.0 s windows every 0.5 s on the joseph:aryan scenes, saved profiles, CPU,
`$HEARING_RESULTS_DIR/T-337_wrong_speaker_audit_20261010-1316.json`). The output is what `tse>mixer` plays at slider 100 under each
arm. `gated (VAD overlap)` counts the extractor's gated windows and, in brackets, how many of them the VAD called overlap. Wrong
windows are split by the oracle class of the 1 s window (target and other stem RMS > 10 % of their speech level).

| Scene | arm | other-dominant | true dropouts | target >= 10 dB down | target >= 6 dB down | gated (VAD overlap) | wrong windows: both voices / other alone | VAD overlap among gated both-voice windows |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| easy | unset | 4.6 s | 0.00 s | 1.0 s | 6.0 s | 21 (3) | 11 (3.2 s) / 4 (1.4 s) | 3 / 13 |
| easy | 20 | 0.0 s | 0.00 s | 6.2 s | 6.4 s | | | |
| easy | 20 + overlap | 4.4 s | 0.00 s | 2.4 s | 6.0 s | | | |
| hard | unset | 5.6 s | 0.00 s | 2.4 s | 10.2 s | 26 (10) | 11 (3.6 s) / 4 (1.6 s) | 4 / 15 |
| hard | 20 | 0.6 s | 0.00 s | 10.2 s | 10.6 s | | | |
| hard | 20 + overlap | 4.0 s | 0.00 s | 5.4 s | 10.6 s | | | |

The live result has two causes:

1. **Both voices present, VAD silent.** In most gated both-voice windows the separator leaves the second voice 15-30 dB under the mix
   in its own stream. So only one stream counts as active, and the window plays at 6 dB with the other voice in the residual.
   11 of the 15 wrong windows have cause `mix_back`.
2. **The other talker alone.** About 30 % of the wrong-speaker time is the other talker speaking alone. Any solo/overlap rule plays
   that at 6 dB by design.

A follow-up has to decide **whose** voice a solo gated window holds, not whether it is solo.

## Adaptive target prototype from confident solo crops, offline A/B on the 4 real-pair rows (T-380, Oct 10)
T-380 (MUSE N1). `eval/adaptive_target.py` (`AdaptiveTargetCandidate`, eval only, no runtime wiring): the closed-set LDA's target class also learns the
unit embedding of the stream it just played, only when the window is confident (`margin_score` >= hold, not vetoed / held / gated) **and** solo
(`StreamVad`: voiced time, no shared time, no overlap). Saved profile pieces and the negative never change; the last 12 crops are kept.
Knob: `--adaptive-target` on `eval.wrong_speaker_audit` (default off; the live pipeline is untouched). Same replay as T-338 (real weights, saved profiles,
1.0 s windows every 0.5 s, `tse>mixer` slider 100, no AGC), laptop-a, MPS, accuracy only (shared slot, no wall-clock number); frozen and adaptive arms share one
separation per window: `.venv/bin/python -m eval.wrong_speaker_audit --profiles $F/profiles --adaptive-target --scene joseph:aryan:$F/t141/joseph/demo-team-easy
--scene aryan:joseph:$F/t141/aryan/demo-team-easy --scene joseph:aryan:$F/t141/joseph/demo-team-hard --scene aryan:joseph:$F/t141/aryan/demo-team-hard`
(`$HEARING_RESULTS_DIR/T-380_adaptive_ab.json`). Wrong-speaker % = other-dominant seconds / clip seconds; target-muted = target-active 200 ms frames >= 10 dB under the input's target part.
The frozen arm reproduces T-338's unset/unset row (4.6 / 1.8 / 5.6 / 2.4 s).

| row | wrong-speaker % frozen -> adaptive | gated windows | target-muted s | windows learned |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| easy joseph | 15.33 -> 6.67 (-8.66 pt) | 21 -> 1 | 1.00 -> 4.80 | 15 |
| easy aryan | 5.96 -> 4.64 (-1.32 pt) | 1 -> 0 | 4.00 -> 8.80 | 12 |
| hard joseph | 18.67 -> 12.00 (-6.67 pt) | 26 -> 17 | 2.40 -> 6.20 | 5 |
| hard aryan | 7.95 -> 7.95 (+0.00 pt) | 3 -> 2 | 7.20 -> 8.60 | 3 |

Kill rule (any row's wrong-speaker % up by more than 1 point): **not triggered**, wrong-speaker never rose. But target-muted time rose on every row (+3.8, +4.8,
+3.8, +1.4 s): fewer gated windows means more confident ducked-residual windows, and the ones that pick the wrong stream mute the target. Verdict: wrong-speaker gain
is real on the joseph rows but paid for in target loss, one scene pair and no listening check; the knob stays off and is not proposed for the demo
config. A natural follow-up is learning only when the pick agrees with the F0 cue, or a target-muted guard.
## Which pair of voices separates best? Aryan / Joseph / Ahmad on one shared passage (T-369, Oct 10)
Question: which two of the three teammates make the best demo pair for the enrolled tier?
**Answer: joseph + ahmad separate best on every measure** (ECAPA cosine 0.03, wrong-speaker 2.1 %, leak -22.3 dB, SI-SDRi +11.2 dB).
If Aryan has to be one of the two, **aryan + ahmad** (3.6 %, -14.3 dB) beats **aryan + joseph** (5.1 %, -13.6 dB), the pair the demo has rehearsed with so far.
The wrong-speaker gaps are small in seconds (0.4 / 0.7 / 1.0 s per 20 s scene) and move in 0.2 s frames. The leak and cosine gaps are larger and point the same way.

**Data.** Only `$HEARING_FIXTURES_DIR/team/<name>/same-01.wav` (enrollment) and `same-02.wav` (test). One 40 s passage read twice by each teammate, recorded Oct 10 on the MacBook Pro microphone, 16 kHz mono. Every person says the same words, so the comparison is fair on content.
**Method** (`.venv/bin/python -m eval.pair_compare --out $HEARING_RESULTS_DIR/T-369_pair_compare.json`, laptop-a `Aryans-MacBook-Pro.local`, Apple M4 Pro, torch `mps`, branch `task/T-369` on main `190d7d77`, about 10 min, accuracy only, no swarm-lock).
(1) ECAPA cosine between the `same-01` enrollment embeddings (`EnrolledCandidate.target_embedding` of the profile clip, as the enrolled tier derives it; the raw whole-clip embedding is also listed).
Short-crop check: 1.0 s crops of each `same-02` every 0.5 s (active speech only), raw ECAPA, nearest enrollment.
(2) For each pair and both target roles, enroll both people from `same-01`; the other is the closed-set negative, as with two saved profiles live. Then build 20 s scenes from `same-02`: target alone 0-5 s, overlap 5-15 s, target alone 15-20 s.
The overlap SIR is 0 or -6 dB (the other talker that much LOUDER). Each scene has no noise or DEMAND `PCAFETER/ch01.wav` at SNR 10 dB under the target, with `eval.knob_sweep`'s levels. The other's speech pieces start half-way through its pool, so the two never read the same words at once.
The real `EnrolledCandidate` replays the scene: MossFormer2_SS_16K + ECAPA pick, closed-set LDA, demo defaults with every T-180 knob env unset (contrast 0, no floors, gated windows silent), 1.0 s windows every 0.5 s.
The output is `stream + confidence floor * (window mix - stream)`, from `eval.knob_sweep.replay` / `compose_output`, the same replay as T-340.
Scores come from `eval.knob_sweep.score`. Wrong-speaker = 200 ms frames where the other talker dominates the output (session_score rule), as a % of the 20 s scene; the other talks only in the 10 s overlap.
Leak = the other stem's least-squares energy in the output vs in the mix. SI-SDRi is given for the whole scene and the overlap section. Target >= 10 dB down = T-326's attenuation check. Gated share = windows where the pick gated.

**ECAPA similarity and 1 s crop confusion.**

| aryan-ahmad | 0.209 | 0.220 | 142 | 0 (0.0 %) | aryan +0.415 / ahmad +0.368 |
| joseph-ahmad | 0.032 | 0.029 | 152 | 0 (0.0 %) | joseph +0.512 / ahmad +0.516 |

| same-02 crops of \ nearest enrollment | aryan | joseph | ahmad |
|---|---|---|---|
| aryan | 65 | 0 | 0 |
| joseph | 0 | 75 | 0 |
| ahmad | 0 | 0 | 77 |

Read: ECAPA alone tells all three apart on clean 1 s crops (0 errors in 217 crops 3-way, 0 in the 434 pairwise decisions). The pairs differ in margin: joseph-ahmad's own-vs-other cosine margin is +0.51, aryan-joseph's +0.35.
Aryan and Joseph are the closest voices of the three (cosine 0.30), and Joseph and Ahmad are nearly orthogonal (0.03).

Recommendation: **demo with joseph + ahmad** for the cleanest separation. If Aryan must be in the pair, use **aryan + ahmad** over aryan + joseph.
The same order holds on cosine, wrong-speaker %, leak and SI-SDRi. Aryan as target is the weakest role in both of his pairs. aryan-joseph's worst scene is aryan target, SIR -6, cafe: 10 % wrong, -8.7 dB leak, +3.7 dB SI-SDRi.

Caveats:
- One 40 s take per person per role (`same-01` enrolls, `same-02` tests), one session, one microphone.
- The scenes are digital mixtures of voices recorded separately: no shared room, no crosstalk, no reverb mismatch between talkers.
- 8 scenes × 20 s per pair. Wrong-speaker moves in 0.2 s frames, so 1 percentage point is one frame.
- The demo knobs (T-180/T-340) were tuned on aryan + joseph scenes. Any bias from that favours aryan + joseph, and it still ranks last.
- Ahmad has no profile takes beyond `same-01/02`. Switching the pair means enrolling him (T-368 re-enrolls Aryan and Joseph only).
- This is the offline replay, not a live `demo_regress` session: no deadlines, AGC or cold start.
No demo code, config or default changed. JSON: `$HEARING_RESULTS_DIR/T-369_pair_compare.json`. Decision record: `docs/decisions/T-PAIR-COMPARE.md`.

## Fine-tuned fixed-order separator live on the Mac (`HEARING_SEP_CKPT` + `HEARING_PICK=fixed`, T-386, Oct 10)

Question: does T-374's fine-tuned fixed-order MossFormer2 checkpoint (slot 0 = Aryan, slot 1 = Joseph), offline wrong-speaker 2.8-14.5 % vs base 33-42 %,
hold up in the live enrolled tier? T-386 wires it in: with `HEARING_SEP_CKPT=$HEARING_MODELS_DIR/T-374/fixed_best.pt` and `HEARING_PICK=fixed`, a
saved profile named `aryan`/`joseph` plays its fixed slot. ECAPA (the LDA/contrast score) only decides the gate and the confidence (`hearing/tse/enrolled.py`
`fixed_target`). Both knobs are off by default. A live calibration clip (no profile name) keeps the ECAPA pick.

**Verdict: ADOPT for the team preset: fine-tuned + fixed pick + `HEARING_GATED_FLOOR_DB=20`.** On the 6 held-out rows it has the best wrong-speaker, leak and SI-SDRi of the four arms on every row
(target-muted s is not best everywhere: it ties base or base+floor 20 on the joseph rows, see the caveat), with wrong-speaker 0.0-4.2 % (mean 1.6 %) against 4.9-31.2 % (mean 15.7 %) for base. Pre-AGC SI-SDRi is +3.9..+12.6 dB (mean +9.2) against +0.7..+5.4 (mean +3.5).
On the 4 T-141 real-pair rows it has the lowest wrong-speaker (mean 3.7 % vs 13.2 % base, 5.6 % base+floor 20), and compute is the same: p95 252-259 ms
and 0 / 232 deadline misses. Caveat: the ECAPA gate still mutes Joseph-as-target. On t141-hard joseph:aryan, 8.2 s of his speech is down >= 10 dB
(6.4 s for base+floor 20), because ECAPA gates 116 of 256 windows of his fixed slot (74 / 265 on base). Next step, not done here: under the fixed pick, gate on
the slot's own energy/VAD instead of ECAPA. Team pair only: T-374 measured the checkpoint breaking a non-team pair (grid-s4:grid-s1 62 % wrong offline).
Do not use it with other profiles. The knob only engages for profiles named `aryan` and `joseph`.
Orchestrator decision (Q-1372): defaults and `demo.sh --team` stay unchanged until Aryan has listened to `~/Desktop/selective-hearing-finetune/` and a live room test passes; the fixed-pick gate fix is a separate follow-up task.

**How it ran.** laptop-a (`Aryans-MacBook-Pro.local`, Apple M4 Pro, MPS, local backend), git `a5946b7c` (T-386 on T-374's branch), the T-284 command per row:
`HEARING_PROFILES_DIR=$HEARING_FIXTURES_DIR/profiles HEARING_REGRESS_NO_BASELINE=1 [knobs] .venv/bin/python scripts/demo_regress.py --modes focus_hq --seconds 30 --no-free --no-baseline --profile team --isolation standard --voice-profile <target>:<other> --clip <scene>`.
Arms: `base` (no knob), `base+floor20` (`HEARING_GATED_FLOOR_DB=20`), `ft+fixed` (`HEARING_SEP_CKPT=$HEARING_MODELS_DIR/T-374/fixed_best.pt HEARING_PICK=fixed`), `ft+fixed+floor20` (both).
- Timing rows (all 4 arms on the T-141 scenes `$HEARING_FIXTURES_DIR/t141/<target>/demo-team-{easy,hard}`) ran in ONE `swarm-lock --exclusive --max-load 5 --wait 1500`
  call, 18:56-19:07 (the lock waited 302 s), 16 rows one after another, all exit 0.
- Quality-only rows on the T-368 held-out scenes (`$HEARING_FIXTURES_DIR/t368/<target>/holdout-{sir0-cafe,sir0-none,sir-6-cafe}`): the two fine-tuned arms ran
  without the lock, 19:07-19:15. The base arms are T-368's `old` / `old+floor20` rows (same command, same `profiles/`, git `5a47d13f`, 16:50-17:12;
  `$HEARING_RESULTS_DIR/T-368_rows_20261010-1712.json`). T-368 has no base+floor20 row for joseph:aryan holdout-sir-6-cafe.
  **Their p95 / deadline-miss columns are not comparable to each other**: the T-368 base rows ran at 1-min load 4-7 with 11-38 misses on some rows, and the
  sir-6-cafe base rows played the enrolled tier only 38-62 % of the time. The fine-tuned rows ran unlocked next to other workers (load 2.5-6.6). Compare quality only.
- Columns: `wrong-speaker` = `other_dominant_s` / 28.4 scored s (`session_score`, includes the 3.9 s cold start); `leak` = the other talker's level out vs in;
  `target-muted s` = target-active 200 ms frames after the cold start whose pre-AGC target part is >= 10 dB below the input's (`eval.knob_sweep.live_atten`, T-326);
  `p95 window ms` / `deadline misses` = the enrolled tier's health at the last tick (`compute_p95_ms`, `overruns + late` / windows; 1.0 s window, 0.5 s hop).
- Rows: `.venv/bin/python -m eval.t386_rows .swarm-run/batch.log .swarm-run/batch_q.log --extra $HEARING_RESULTS_DIR/T-368_rows_20261010-1712.json --out $HEARING_RESULTS_DIR/T-386_rows.json`.
  Listening A/B (holdout-sir0-cafe, both targets, level-matched): `~/Desktop/selective-hearing-finetune/` (`scripts/t386_export_ab.py`).

Means per arm (t141: 4 rows, in the lock; holdout: 6 rows, 5 for base+floor20):
